"""Memory subsystem: SQLite store, working memory, and checkpointing."""


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\memory\checkpoint.py
================================================================================

"""Checkpoint and resume — persist agent state after each step.

Checkpoints are stored as JSON files alongside the SQLite database
so a crashed or paused run can resume from the last completed step.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


@dataclass
class Checkpoint:
    """Serialisable snapshot of the agent state at a given step."""

    run_id: str
    goal: str
    step_index: int
    plan: list[str]
    scratchpad: str
    status: str
    messages_snapshot: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Checkpoint:
        return cls(**{k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in cls.__dataclass_fields__})


class CheckpointStore:
    """File-based checkpoint storage."""

    def __init__(self, directory: Path | str) -> None:
        self._dir = Path(directory)
        self._dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def _path(self, run_id: str) -> Path:
        safe = "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in "-_" else "_" for c in run_id)[:80]
        return self._dir / f"checkpoint_{safe}.json"

    def save(self, checkpoint: Checkpoint) -> Path:
        """Save a checkpoint, overwriting any previous one for the same run."""
        path = self._path(checkpoint.run_id)
        path.write_text(json.dumps(checkpoint.to_dict(), indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        return path

    def load(self, run_id: str) -> Checkpoint | None:
        """Load the most recent checkpoint for a run, or None."""
        path = self._path(run_id)
        if not path.exists():
            return None
        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return Checkpoint.from_dict(data)

    def delete(self, run_id: str) -> None:
        path = self._path(run_id)
        if path.exists():
            path.unlink()

    def list_checkpoints(self) -> list[str]:
        """Return run IDs that have checkpoints."""
        return [
            p.stem.removeprefix("checkpoint_")
            for p in self._dir.glob("checkpoint_*.json")
        ]


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\memory\store.py
================================================================================

"""Persistent memory store backed by SQLite.

Stores:
* **Runs** — goal, status, timestamps, and summary for each agent run.
* **Steps** — every observation → decision → action → result within a run.
* **Skills** — verified procedures saved for reuse (procedural memory).

The schema is append-only by default to preserve a full audit trail.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any


def _utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


class RunStatus(str, Enum):
    PLANNING = "planning"
    EXECUTING = "executing"
    VERIFYING = "verifying"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    AWAITING_HUMAN = "awaiting_human"
    KILLED = "killed"


@dataclass
class StepRecord:
    """A single step in an agent run."""

    step_index: int
    action_type: str  # e.g. "tool_call", "plan", "observe", "verify"
    tool_name: str | None = None
    tool_params: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    observation: str = ""
    decision: str = ""
    result_output: str = ""
    result_success: bool | None = None
    verification_status: str = "skipped"
    verification_message: str = ""
    error: str | None = None
    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=_utc_now)
    result_metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class RunRecord:
    """Summary of a complete agent run."""

    run_id: str
    goal: str
    status: RunStatus
    steps: list[StepRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
    plan: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    summary: str = ""
    created_at: str = field(default_factory=_utc_now)
    completed_at: str | None = None
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class SkillRecord:
    """A reusable procedure extracted from a successful run."""

    skill_id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    procedure: list[dict[str, Any]]
    source_run_id: str
    created_at: str = field(default_factory=_utc_now)
    success_count: int = 0
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


_SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (
    run_id       TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    goal         TEXT NOT NULL,
    status       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'planning',
    plan         TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    summary      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    created_at   TEXT NOT NULL,
    completed_at TEXT,
    metadata     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS steps (
    id                   INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    run_id               TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES runs(run_id),
    step_index           INTEGER NOT NULL,
    action_type          TEXT NOT NULL,
    tool_name            TEXT,
    tool_params          TEXT,
    observation          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    decision             TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    result_output        TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    result_success       INTEGER,
    verification_status  TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'skipped',
    verification_message TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    error                TEXT,
    timestamp            TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_steps_run ON steps(run_id, step_index);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS skills (
    skill_id      TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name          TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    description   TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    procedure     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    source_run_id TEXT,
    created_at    TEXT NOT NULL,
    success_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tags          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'
);
"""


class MemoryStore:
    """SQLite-backed persistent memory."""

    def __init__(self, db_path: Path | str) -> None:
        self._db_path = Path(db_path)
        self._db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._init_db()

    # ── Connection management ────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _init_db(self) -> None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.executescript(_SCHEMA)
            columns = {row["name"] for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(steps)")}
            if "result_metadata" not in columns:
                conn.execute("ALTER TABLE steps ADD COLUMN result_metadata TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'")

    @contextmanager
    def _connect(self) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self._db_path), isolation_level="DEFERRED")
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
        try:
            yield conn
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    # ── Runs ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def create_run(self, run_id: str, goal: str) -> RunRecord:
        record = RunRecord(run_id=run_id, goal=goal, status=RunStatus.PLANNING)
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO runs (run_id, goal, status, plan, summary, created_at, metadata) "
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (record.run_id, record.goal, record.status.value,
                 json.dumps(record.plan), record.summary, record.created_at,
                 json.dumps(record.metadata)),
            )
        return record

    def update_run_status(self, run_id: str, status: RunStatus, *, summary: str | None = None) -> None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            if summary is not None:
                conn.execute(
                    "UPDATE runs SET status=?, summary=?, completed_at=? WHERE run_id=?",
                    (status.value, summary, _utc_now() if status in (RunStatus.COMPLETED, RunStatus.FAILED, RunStatus.KILLED) else None, run_id),
                )
            else:
                conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET status=? WHERE run_id=?", (status.value, run_id))

    def update_run_plan(self, run_id: str, plan: list[str]) -> None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET plan=? WHERE run_id=?", (json.dumps(plan), run_id))

    def update_run_metadata(self, run_id: str, values: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            row = conn.execute("SELECT metadata FROM runs WHERE run_id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
            metadata = json.loads(row["metadata"])
            metadata.update(values)
            conn.execute("UPDATE runs SET metadata=? WHERE run_id=?", (json.dumps(metadata), run_id))

    def get_run(self, run_id: str) -> RunRecord | None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM runs WHERE run_id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                return None
            steps = self._load_steps(conn, run_id)
            return RunRecord(
                run_id=row["run_id"], goal=row["goal"],
                status=RunStatus(row["status"]),
                plan=json.loads(row["plan"]),
                summary=row["summary"],
                steps=steps,
                created_at=row["created_at"],
                completed_at=row["completed_at"],
                metadata=json.loads(row["metadata"]),
            )

    def list_runs(self, limit: int = 20) -> list[RunRecord]:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM runs ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,)
            ).fetchall()
            return [
                RunRecord(
                    run_id=r["run_id"], goal=r["goal"],
                    status=RunStatus(r["status"]),
                    plan=json.loads(r["plan"]),
                    summary=r["summary"], created_at=r["created_at"],
                    completed_at=r["completed_at"],
                    metadata=json.loads(r["metadata"]),
                )
                for r in rows
            ]

    # ── Steps ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def record_step(self, run_id: str, step: StepRecord) -> None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO steps "
                "(run_id, step_index, action_type, tool_name, tool_params, "
                "observation, decision, result_output, result_success, "
                "verification_status, verification_message, error, timestamp, result_metadata) "
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (run_id, step.step_index, step.action_type, step.tool_name,
                 json.dumps(step.tool_params) if step.tool_params else None,
                 step.observation, step.decision, step.result_output,
                 int(step.result_success) if step.result_success is not None else None,
                 step.verification_status, step.verification_message,
                 step.error, step.timestamp, json.dumps(step.result_metadata)),
            )

    def _load_steps(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, run_id: str) -> list[StepRecord]:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM steps WHERE run_id=? ORDER BY step_index", (run_id,)
        ).fetchall()
        return [
            StepRecord(
                step_index=r["step_index"],
                action_type=r["action_type"],
                tool_name=r["tool_name"],
                tool_params=json.loads(r["tool_params"]) if r["tool_params"] else None,
                observation=r["observation"],
                decision=r["decision"],
                result_output=r["result_output"],
                result_success=bool(r["result_success"]) if r["result_success"] is not None else None,
                verification_status=r["verification_status"],
                verification_message=r["verification_message"],
                error=r["error"],
                timestamp=r["timestamp"],
                result_metadata=json.loads(r["result_metadata"]),
            )
            for r in rows
        ]

    # ── Skills (procedural memory) ───────────────────────────────────────

    def save_skill(self, skill: SkillRecord) -> None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO skills "
                "(skill_id, name, description, procedure, source_run_id, "
                "created_at, success_count, tags) "
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (skill.skill_id, skill.name, skill.description,
                 json.dumps(skill.procedure), skill.source_run_id,
                 skill.created_at, skill.success_count,
                 json.dumps(skill.tags)),
            )

    def find_skill(self, name: str) -> SkillRecord | None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM skills WHERE name=?", (name,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                return None
            return SkillRecord(
                skill_id=row["skill_id"], name=row["name"],
                description=row["description"],
                procedure=json.loads(row["procedure"]),
                source_run_id=row["source_run_id"],
                created_at=row["created_at"],
                success_count=row["success_count"],
                tags=json.loads(row["tags"]),
            )

    def list_skills(self) -> list[SkillRecord]:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM skills ORDER BY name").fetchall()
            return [
                SkillRecord(
                    skill_id=r["skill_id"], name=r["name"],
                    description=r["description"],
                    procedure=json.loads(r["procedure"]),
                    source_run_id=r["source_run_id"],
                    created_at=r["created_at"],
                    success_count=r["success_count"],
                    tags=json.loads(r["tags"]),
                )
                for r in rows
            ]


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\memory\working.py
================================================================================

"""Working memory — manages the rolling context window for the agent.

The working memory is responsible for:
* Maintaining the conversation messages list within the token budget.
* Compacting long observations into summaries.
* Inserting the current plan and scratchpad into context.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any


@dataclass
class WorkingMemory:
    """Rolling context window that stays within a token budget.

    This is a simplified token estimator that counts characters ÷ 4
    as a rough approximation.  Replace with a proper tokeniser when
    the model is known.
    """

    max_tokens: int = 8192
    system_prompt: str = ""

    # ── Internal state ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list, init=False)
    _plan: list[str] = field(default_factory=list, init=False)
    _scratchpad: str = field(default="", init=False)
    _latest_observation: dict[str, Any] | None = field(default=None, init=False)
    _latest_snapshot: str | None = field(default=None, init=False)

    # ── Token estimation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @staticmethod
    def _estimate_tokens(text: str) -> int:
        """Rough token count: chars / 4."""
        return max(1, len(text) // 4)

    def _total_tokens(self) -> int:
        total = self._estimate_tokens(self.system_prompt)
        for msg in self._messages:
            total += self._estimate_tokens(str(msg.get("content", "")))
        return total

    # ── Context building ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def set_plan(self, plan: list[str]) -> None:
        self._plan = list(plan)

    def set_scratchpad(self, text: str) -> None:
        self._scratchpad = text

    def add_message(self, role: str, content: str, **extra: Any) -> None:
        """Append a message, compacting old messages if over budget."""
        msg: dict[str, Any] = {"role": role, "content": content, **extra}
        self._messages.append(msg)
        self._compact_if_needed()

    # def add_tool_result(self, tool_call_id: str, content: str) -> None:
    #     """Append a tool result message."""
    #     self._messages.append({
    #         "role": "tool",
    #         "content": self._truncate(content, max_chars=4000),
    #         "tool_call_id": tool_call_id,
    #     })
    #     self._compact_if_needed()
    def add_tool_result(self, tool_name: str, content: str, *, metadata=None, success=True) -> None:
        """Append a tool result message."""
        message = {
            "role": "tool",
            "tool_name": tool_name,
            "content": self._truncate(content, max_chars=4000),
        }
        if success and metadata and metadata.get("action") == "observe" and metadata.get("snapshot_id"):
            if self._latest_observation is not None:
                self._latest_observation["content"] = (
                    f"[Observation {self._latest_snapshot} elided; superseded by {metadata['snapshot_id']}]."
                )
            self._latest_observation = message
            self._latest_snapshot = metadata["snapshot_id"]
        self._messages.append(message)
        self._compact_if_needed()

    def get_messages(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return the full message list including the system prompt."""
        system: dict[str, Any] = {"role": "system", "content": self._build_system()}
        return [system] + list(self._messages)

    def clear(self) -> None:
        self._messages.clear()
        self._plan.clear()
        self._scratchpad = ""
        self._latest_observation = None
        self._latest_snapshot = None

    @property
    def message_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self._messages)

    # ── Internal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _build_system(self) -> str:
        parts = [self.system_prompt]
        if self._plan:
            plan_text = "\n".join(f"  {i+1}. {step}" for i, step in enumerate(self._plan))
            parts.append(f"\n\n## Current Plan\n{plan_text}")
        if self._scratchpad:
            parts.append(f"\n\n## Scratchpad\n{self._scratchpad}")
        return "\n".join(parts)

    def _compact_if_needed(self) -> None:
        """Drop oldest non-system messages when over the token budget."""
        max_iterations = len(self._messages) + 5  # Guard against infinite loops.
        iterations = 0
        while self._total_tokens() > self.max_tokens and len(self._messages) > 2:
            iterations += 1
            if iterations > max_iterations:
                break
            # Keep the first message (usually the goal) and the last few.
            removed = self._messages.pop(1)
            # Insert a brief summary of what was dropped (only once).
            if self._messages and self._messages[0].get("role") == "user":
                pass  # keep the goal message
            summary_note = f"[Earlier message removed for context management: {removed.get('role', '?')}]"
            # Don't insert summary if we already have one.
            if not any("[Earlier message removed" in str(m.get("content", "")) for m in self._messages[:2]):
                self._messages.insert(1, {"role": "system", "content": summary_note})

    @staticmethod
    def _truncate(text: str, max_chars: int = 4000) -> str:
        if len(text) <= max_chars:
            return text
        half = max_chars // 2
        return text[:half] + f"\n…[{len(text) - max_chars} chars truncated]…\n" + text[-half:]


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\models.py

"""Ollama model manager with role-based VRAM-aware scheduling.

The manager maps logical roles (planner, vision, embed, …) to concrete
Ollama models and ensures only one large model is resident at a time within
the VRAM budget.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import httpx

from local_agent.config import AgentConfig, ModelRoleConfig

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ── Response types ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@dataclass
class ToolCall:
    """A single tool invocation returned by the model."""

    id: str
    name: str
    arguments: dict[str, Any]


@dataclass
class ChatResponse:
    """Parsed response from an Ollama /api/chat call."""

    content: str
    tool_calls: list[ToolCall]
    model: str
    role: str
    done: bool
    thinking: str = ""
    done_reason: str = ""
    total_duration_ns: int = 0
    eval_count: int = 0
    prompt_eval_count: int = 0

    @property
    def has_tool_calls(self) -> bool:
        return len(self.tool_calls) > 0


@dataclass
class EmbedResponse:
    """Parsed response from an Ollama /api/embed call."""

    embeddings: list[list[float]]
    model: str


# ── Model Manager ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ModelManager:
    """Manages Ollama model loading and inference, one model at a time.

    This is the single interface the agent core uses for all LLM work.
    It handles:
    * Role → model mapping via :class:`AgentConfig`.
    * Model loading / unloading to stay within the VRAM budget.
    * Chat completions (with optional tool calling).
    * Embedding requests.
    """

    def __init__(self, config: AgentConfig) -> None:
        self._config = config
        self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(
            base_url=config.ollama_base_url,
            timeout=httpx.Timeout(connect=15, read=360, write=60, pool=10),
        )
        self._current_model: str | None = None
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
        self._no_think: set[str] = set()  # models that reject the `think` option

    # ── Public API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def chat(
        self,
        role: str,
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
        *,
        tools: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        temperature: float | None = None,
        max_tokens: int | None = None,
    ) -> ChatResponse:
        """Send a chat completion request using the model for *role*."""
        role_cfg = self._role_config(role)
        await self._ensure_model_loaded(role_cfg.model)

        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": role_cfg.model,
            "messages": messages,
            "stream": False,
            "options": {
                "temperature": temperature if temperature is not None else role_cfg.temperature,
                "num_ctx": role_cfg.context_length,
            },
        }
        if max_tokens is not None:
            payload["options"]["num_predict"] = max_tokens
        if tools:
            payload["tools"] = tools
        # Control thinking explicitly (executor: off, planner: on). Models that do not
        # support the option are remembered and retried without it.
        if role_cfg.model not in self._no_think:
            payload["think"] = role_cfg.thinking

        resp = await self._client.post("/api/chat", json=payload)
        if resp.status_code == 400 and "think" in payload and "think" in resp.text.lower():
            logger.info("Model %s does not support 'think'; retrying without it.", role_cfg.model)
            self._no_think.add(role_cfg.model)
            payload.pop("think")
            resp = await self._client.post("/api/chat", json=payload)
        resp.raise_for_status()
        data = resp.json()

        # Parse tool calls if present.
        tool_calls: list[ToolCall] = []
        msg = data.get("message", {})
        for i, tc in enumerate(msg.get("tool_calls", [])):
            fn = tc.get("function", {})
            tool_calls.append(ToolCall(
                id=tc.get("id", f"call_{i}"),
                name=fn.get("name", ""),
                arguments=fn.get("arguments", {}),
            ))

        prompt_tokens = data.get("prompt_eval_count", 0) or 0
        gen_tokens = data.get("eval_count", 0) or 0
        done_reason = data.get("done_reason", "") or ""
        content_text = msg.get("content", "") or ""
        thinking_text = msg.get("thinking", "") or ""
        logger.info(
            "chat[%s] %s: prompt=%d/%d tok, generated=%d tok, done_reason=%s, "
            "content=%d chars, thinking=%d chars, tool_calls=%d",
            role, role_cfg.model, prompt_tokens, role_cfg.context_length, gen_tokens,
            done_reason or "?", len(content_text), len(thinking_text), len(tool_calls),
        )
        if prompt_tokens >= 0.9 * role_cfg.context_length:
            logger.warning(
                "Prompt used %d of %d context tokens (role=%s). Ollama silently truncates input "
                "beyond num_ctx, which breaks tool calling. Raise context_length or shrink the "
                "prompt/tools.", prompt_tokens, role_cfg.context_length, role,
            )
        if done_reason == "length":
            logger.warning("Generation for role=%s stopped at the token/context limit.", role)

        return ChatResponse(
            content=content_text,
            tool_calls=tool_calls,
            model=data.get("model", role_cfg.model),
            role=msg.get("role", "assistant"),
            done=data.get("done", True),
            thinking=thinking_text,
                done_reason=done_reason,
            total_duration_ns=data.get("total_duration", 0),
            eval_count=data.get("eval_count", 0),
            prompt_eval_count=data.get("prompt_eval_count", 0),
        )

    async def embed(self, texts: list[str], *, role: str = "embed") -> EmbedResponse:
        """Compute embeddings for a list of texts."""
        role_cfg = self._role_config(role)
        await self._ensure_model_loaded(role_cfg.model)

        resp = await self._client.post("/api/embed", json={
            "model": role_cfg.model,
            "input": texts,
        })
        resp.raise_for_status()
        data = resp.json()

        return EmbedResponse(
            embeddings=data.get("embeddings", []),
            model=data.get("model", role_cfg.model),
        )

    async def list_running(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """List models currently loaded in Ollama."""
        resp = await self._client.get("/api/ps")
        resp.raise_for_status()
        return resp.json().get("models", [])

    async def health_check(self) -> bool:
        """Return True if Ollama is reachable."""
        try:
            resp = await self._client.get("/api/tags")
            return resp.status_code == 200
        except httpx.HTTPError:
            return False

    async def close(self) -> None:
        await self._client.aclose()

    # ── Internal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _role_config(self, role: str) -> ModelRoleConfig:
        cfg = self._config.model_roles.get(role)
        if cfg is None:
            raise ValueError(f"No model configured for role '{role}'. "
                             f"Available roles: {list(self._config.model_roles)}")
        return cfg

    async def _ensure_model_loaded(self, model: str) -> None:
        """Load *model* if it is not already the current resident model.

        Ollama handles loading/unloading internally, but we track which model
        we last used so we can log swaps and avoid unnecessary keep-alive
        requests.
        """
        async with self._lock:
            if self._current_model == model:
                return

            if self._current_model is not None:
                logger.info("Model swap: %s → %s", self._current_model, model)
                # Ask Ollama to unload the previous model by setting keep_alive to 0.
                try:
                    await self._client.post("/api/generate", json={
                        "model": self._current_model,
                        "keep_alive": 0,
                    })
                except httpx.HTTPError:
                    logger.warning("Failed to unload model %s", self._current_model)

            # Pre-load the new model with a keep-alive.
            logger.info("Loading model: %s", model)
            try:
                await self._client.post("/api/generate", json={
                    "model": model,
                    "keep_alive": "10m",
                })
            except httpx.HTTPError:
                logger.warning("Failed to pre-load model %s — will try on first call", model)

            self._current_model = model

================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\rag\__init__.py

"""Document intelligence — parse, chunk, embed, and search documents.

This module implements the RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) pipeline
described in the docx:
* Parse documents (PDF, Office, HTML, text) using Docling.
* Chunk text into semantically meaningful segments.
* Embed chunks using bge-m3 via Ollama.
* Store embeddings in a local vector database (LanceDB).
* Retrieve relevant chunks with hybrid BM25 + dense search.
* Return answers grounded in sources with page references.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import logging
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ── Data types ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@dataclass
class DocumentChunk:
    """A single chunk of text from a parsed document."""

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    document_path: str
    text: str
    page_number: int | None = None
    section: str = ""
    chunk_index: int = 0
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class SearchHit:
    """A single search result from the RAG pipeline."""

    chunk: DocumentChunk
    score: float
    source_reference: str = ""  # e.g. "document.pdf, page 3"


@dataclass
class RAGResponse:
    """Response from a RAG query."""

    query: str
    hits: list[SearchHit]
    context_text: str  # Combined text from top hits for LLM context.


# ── Document parser ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class DocumentParser:
    """Parse documents into text chunks.

    Supports:
    * Plain text (.txt, .md, .py, .json, .csv, etc.)
    * PDF (via Docling when available, otherwise fallback)
    * HTML (basic extraction)

    Docling, Marker, and other heavy parsers are lazy-imported so the
    module works without them for basic text files.
    """

    def parse(self, path: Path | str) -> list[DocumentChunk]:
        """Parse a document and return chunks."""
        from local_agent.security.paths import document_path
        path = document_path(path)
        if not path.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Document not found: {path}")

        doc_id = self._doc_id(path)
        suffix = path.suffix.lower()

        if suffix in (".txt", ".md", ".py", ".js", ".ts", ".sh", ".yaml", ".yml",
                       ".toml", ".json", ".csv", ".log", ".cfg", ".ini", ".xml"):
            return self._parse_text(path, doc_id)
        elif suffix == ".pdf":
            return self._parse_pdf(path, doc_id)
        elif suffix in (".html", ".htm"):
            return self._parse_html(path, doc_id)
        elif suffix in (".docx", ".xlsx", ".pptx"):
            return self._parse_office(path, doc_id)
        else:
            # Try as plain text.
            return self._parse_text(path, doc_id)

    def _doc_id(self, path: Path) -> str:
        content_hash = hashlib.md5(str(path.resolve()).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
        return f"{path.stem}_{content_hash}"

    def _parse_text(self, path: Path, doc_id: str) -> list[DocumentChunk]:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        return self._chunk_text(text, doc_id, str(path))

    def _parse_pdf(self, path: Path, doc_id: str) -> list[DocumentChunk]:
        """Parse PDF — try Docling first, fall back to basic extraction."""
        try:
            from docling.document_converter import DocumentConverter
            converter = DocumentConverter()
            result = converter.convert(str(path))
            text = result.document.export_to_markdown()
            chunks = self._chunk_text(text, doc_id, str(path))
            logger.info("Parsed PDF with Docling: %s (%d chunks)", path.name, len(chunks))
            return chunks
        except ImportError:
            logger.info("Docling not available, trying PyPDF2 fallback for %s", path.name)

        # Fallback: try PyPDF2 or basic extraction.
        try:
            import PyPDF2
            reader = PyPDF2.PdfReader(str(path))
            chunks: list[DocumentChunk] = []
            for i, page in enumerate(reader.pages):
                page_text = page.extract_text() or ""
                if page_text.strip():
                    for j, chunk_text in enumerate(self._split_into_chunks(page_text)):
                        chunks.append(DocumentChunk(
                            chunk_id=f"{doc_id}_p{i+1}_c{j}",
                            document_id=doc_id,
                            document_path=str(path),
                            text=chunk_text,
                            page_number=i + 1,
                            chunk_index=len(chunks),
                        ))
            return chunks or self._chunk_text(f"[PDF with {len(reader.pages)} pages — text extraction failed]", doc_id, str(path))
        except ImportError:
            logger.warning("No PDF parser available (install docling or PyPDF2)")
            return self._chunk_text(f"[PDF file: {path.name} — no parser available]", doc_id, str(path))

    def _parse_html(self, path: Path, doc_id: str) -> list[DocumentChunk]:
        """Parse HTML — strip tags for text content."""
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        # Basic tag stripping.
        clean = re.sub(r"<script[^>]*>.*?</script>", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE)
        clean = re.sub(r"<style[^>]*>.*?</style>", "", clean, flags=re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE)
        clean = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", clean)
        clean = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", clean).strip()
        return self._chunk_text(clean, doc_id, str(path))

    def _parse_office(self, path: Path, doc_id: str) -> list[DocumentChunk]:
        """Parse Office documents — try Docling, fall back to python-docx/openpyxl."""
        try:
            from docling.document_converter import DocumentConverter
            converter = DocumentConverter()
            result = converter.convert(str(path))
            text = result.document.export_to_markdown()
            return self._chunk_text(text, doc_id, str(path))
        except ImportError:
            pass

        suffix = path.suffix.lower()
        if suffix == ".docx":
            try:
                import docx
                doc = docx.Document(str(path))
                text = "\n".join(p.text for p in doc.paragraphs if p.text.strip())
                return self._chunk_text(text, doc_id, str(path))
            except ImportError:
                pass

        logger.warning("No parser for %s (install docling or python-docx)", suffix)
        return self._chunk_text(f"[Office file: {path.name} — no parser available]", doc_id, str(path))

    def _chunk_text(
        self, text: str, doc_id: str, doc_path: str
    ) -> list[DocumentChunk]:
        """Split text into chunks and create DocumentChunk objects."""
        raw_chunks = self._split_into_chunks(text)
        return [
            DocumentChunk(
                chunk_id=f"{doc_id}_c{i}",
                document_id=doc_id,
                document_path=doc_path,
                text=chunk,
                chunk_index=i,
            )
            for i, chunk in enumerate(raw_chunks)
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _split_into_chunks(
        text: str,
        max_chunk_size: int = 512,
        overlap: int = 64,
    ) -> list[str]:
        """Split text into overlapping chunks by paragraph/sentence boundaries."""
        if not text.strip():
            return []

        # Split on double newlines (paragraphs) first.
        paragraphs = re.split(r"\n\s*\n", text)
        chunks: list[str] = []
        current: list[str] = []
        current_len = 0

        for para in paragraphs:
            para = para.strip()
            if not para:
                continue

            para_len = len(para.split())
            if current_len + para_len > max_chunk_size and current:
                chunks.append("\n\n".join(current))
                # Keep overlap from the last paragraph.
                if overlap > 0 and current:
                    overlap_text = current[-1]
                    words = overlap_text.split()
                    if len(words) > overlap:
                        overlap_text = " ".join(words[-overlap:])
                    current = [overlap_text]
                    current_len = len(overlap_text.split())
                else:
                    current = []
                    current_len = 0

            current.append(para)
            current_len += para_len

        if current:
            chunks.append("\n\n".join(current))

        return chunks if chunks else [text[:max_chunk_size * 5]]


# ── Vector store interface ───────────────────────────────────────────────

class VectorStore:
    """Local vector store backed by LanceDB (when available) or a simple
    in-memory brute-force search as fallback.

    The store persists document chunks with their embeddings and supports
    hybrid retrieval (dense + keyword matching).
    """

    def __init__(self, db_path: Path | str = "data/vectordb") -> None:
        self._db_path = Path(db_path)
        self._db_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._db = None
        self._table = None
        self._fallback_store: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._use_lancedb = False

        try:
            import lancedb
            self._db = lancedb.connect(str(self._db_path))
            self._use_lancedb = True
            logger.info("VectorStore: using LanceDB at %s", self._db_path)
        except ImportError:
            logger.info("VectorStore: LanceDB not available, using in-memory fallback")

    def add_chunks(
        self, chunks: list[DocumentChunk], embeddings: list[list[float]]
    ) -> int:
        """Add document chunks with their embeddings to the store."""
        if len(chunks) != len(embeddings):
            raise ValueError("Number of chunks and embeddings must match.")

        records = []
        for chunk, embedding in zip(chunks, embeddings):
            records.append({
                "chunk_id": chunk.chunk_id,
                "document_id": chunk.document_id,
                "document_path": chunk.document_path,
                "text": chunk.text,
                "page_number": chunk.page_number,
                "section": chunk.section,
                "chunk_index": chunk.chunk_index,
                "vector": embedding,
            })

        if self._use_lancedb:
            try:
                if self._table is None:
                    tables = self._db.table_names()
                    if "chunks" in tables:
                        self._table = self._db.open_table("chunks")
                        self._table.add(records)
                    else:
                        self._table = self._db.create_table("chunks", records)
                else:
                    self._table.add(records)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning("LanceDB add failed, using fallback: %s", exc)
                self._fallback_store.extend(records)
        else:
            self._fallback_store.extend(records)

        return len(records)

    def search(
        self, query_embedding: list[float], top_k: int = 5
    ) -> list[SearchHit]:
        """Search for the most relevant chunks using vector similarity."""
        if self._use_lancedb and self._table is not None:
            try:
                results = (
                    self._table.search(query_embedding)
                    .limit(top_k)
                    .to_list()
                )
                return [
                    SearchHit(
                        chunk=DocumentChunk(
                            chunk_id=r["chunk_id"],
                            document_id=r["document_id"],
                            document_path=r["document_path"],
                            text=r["text"],
                            page_number=r.get("page_number"),
                            section=r.get("section", ""),
                            chunk_index=r.get("chunk_index", 0),
                        ),
                        score=1.0 - r.get("_distance", 0),
                        source_reference=self._make_source_ref(r),
                    )
                    for r in results
                ]
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning("LanceDB search failed: %s", exc)

        # Fallback: brute-force cosine similarity.
        return self._fallback_search(query_embedding, top_k)

    def _fallback_search(
        self, query_embedding: list[float], top_k: int
    ) -> list[SearchHit]:
        """In-memory brute-force search using cosine similarity."""
        import math

        def cosine_sim(a: list[float], b: list[float]) -> float:
            dot = sum(x * y for x, y in zip(a, b))
            norm_a = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in a))
            norm_b = math.sqrt(sum(x * x for x in b))
            if norm_a == 0 or norm_b == 0:
                return 0.0
            return dot / (norm_a * norm_b)

        scored = []
        for record in self._fallback_store:
            score = cosine_sim(query_embedding, record["vector"])
            scored.append((score, record))

        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)

        return [
            SearchHit(
                chunk=DocumentChunk(
                    chunk_id=r["chunk_id"],
                    document_id=r["document_id"],
                    document_path=r["document_path"],
                    text=r["text"],
                    page_number=r.get("page_number"),
                    section=r.get("section", ""),
                    chunk_index=r.get("chunk_index", 0),
                ),
                score=score,
                source_reference=self._make_source_ref(r),
            )
            for score, r in scored[:top_k]
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _make_source_ref(record: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        path = Path(record.get("document_path", "unknown"))
        page = record.get("page_number")
        ref = path.name
        if page is not None:
            ref += f", page {page}"
        return ref

    @property
    def chunk_count(self) -> int:
        if self._use_lancedb and self._table is not None:
            try:
                return self._table.count_rows()
            except Exception:
                pass
        return len(self._fallback_store)


# ── RAG pipeline ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class RAGPipeline:
    """End-to-end RAG pipeline: parse → chunk → embed → store → retrieve.

    Usage::

        rag = RAGPipeline(model_manager)
        await rag.ingest("/path/to/document.pdf")
        response = await rag.query("What is the main finding?")
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model_manager: Any,
        vector_store: VectorStore | None = None,
        parser: DocumentParser | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._model = model_manager
        self._store = vector_store or VectorStore()
        self._parser = parser or DocumentParser()

    async def ingest(self, path: str | Path) -> int:
        """Parse a document, embed it, and store it.  Returns chunk count."""
        from local_agent.security.paths import document_path
        path = document_path(path)
        chunks = self._parser.parse(path)
        if not chunks:
            logger.warning("No chunks extracted from %s", path)
            return 0

        # Embed all chunks.
        texts = [c.text for c in chunks]
        response = await self._model.embed(texts)
        embeddings = response.embeddings

        if len(embeddings) != len(chunks):
            logger.warning(
                "Embedding count mismatch: %d chunks, %d embeddings",
                len(chunks), len(embeddings),
            )
            min_len = min(len(chunks), len(embeddings))
            chunks = chunks[:min_len]
            embeddings = embeddings[:min_len]

        added = self._store.add_chunks(chunks, embeddings)
        logger.info("Ingested %s: %d chunks stored", path.name, added)
        return added

    async def query(
        self, question: str, top_k: int = 5
    ) -> RAGResponse:
        """Query the document store and return relevant chunks."""
        # Embed the question.
        response = await self._model.embed([question])
        if not response.embeddings:
            return RAGResponse(query=question, hits=[], context_text="")

        query_embedding = response.embeddings[0]
        hits = self._store.search(query_embedding, top_k=top_k)

        # Build context text with source citations.
        context_parts: list[str] = []
        for i, hit in enumerate(hits, 1):
            context_parts.append(
                f"[Source {i}: {hit.source_reference}]\n{hit.chunk.text}"
            )

        context_text = "\n\n---\n\n".join(context_parts)

        return RAGResponse(
            query=question,
            hits=hits,
            context_text=context_text,
        )

    @property
    def document_count(self) -> int:
        return self._store.chunk_count

"""Sandbox subsystem: client for communicating with the in-sandbox tool server."""

"""Sandbox client — communicates with the FastAPI tool server inside Docker.

The sandbox client is the *only* way the agent executes actions.  All
tool implementations delegate to this client, which in turn calls
the tool server running inside the Docker container.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
from typing import Any, Literal

import httpx

from local_agent.tools.base import ToolResult

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SandboxClient:
    """HTTP client for the sandbox tool server."""

    def __init__(self, base_url: str = "http://localhost:9000", *, admin_token: str | None = None) -> None:
        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self._browser_approval_token = None
        self._admin_token = admin_token if admin_token is not None else os.environ.get("XALGV_SANDBOX_ADMIN_TOKEN")
        self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(
            base_url=self._base_url,
            timeout=httpx.Timeout(connect=5, read=120, write=10, pool=5),
        )

    # ── Shell ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def execute_shell(
        self,
        command: str,
        working_dir: str = "/workspace",
        timeout: int = 30,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        """Execute a shell command in the sandbox."""
        return await self._post("/tools/shell", {
            "command": command,
            "working_dir": working_dir,
            "timeout": timeout,
        }, headers={"X-Sandbox-Admin": self._admin_token} if self._admin_token else None)

    # ── Files ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def read_file(self, path: str, *, max_lines: int = 200) -> ToolResult:
        """Read file contents from the sandbox."""
        return await self._post("/tools/read_file", {
            "path": path,
            "max_lines": max_lines,
        })

    async def write_file(self, path: str, content: str, append: bool = False) -> ToolResult:
        """Write (or append to) a file in the sandbox."""
        return await self._post("/tools/write_file", {
            "path": path,
            "content": content,
            "append": append,
        })

    async def list_files(self, path: str = "/workspace") -> ToolResult:
        """List files in a directory in the sandbox."""
        return await self._post("/tools/list_files", {"path": path})

    # ── Screenshot (for later phases) ────────────────────────────────────

    async def screenshot(self) -> ToolResult:
        """Take a screenshot of the sandbox desktop."""
        return await self._post("/tools/screenshot", {})

    # ── Browser automation ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def browser_verify(self, checks: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> ToolResult:
        """Trusted caller checks; deliberately absent from the model tool registry."""
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/verify", {"checks": checks})

    async def browser_image(self, path: str) -> ToolResult:
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/image", {"path": path})

    async def browser_authorize(self, tool: str, args: dict[str, Any], admin_token: str) -> ToolResult:
        """Call only after human review; neither this method nor its secret is model-exposed."""
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/authorize", {"tool": tool, "args": args},
                                headers={"X-XBrowser-Admin": admin_token})

    def use_browser_approval(self, token: str):
        self._browser_approval_token = token

    async def browser_press(self, key: str) -> ToolResult:
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/press", {"key": key})

    async def browser_scroll(
        self, direction: str = "down", pixels: int = 600, wait_ms: int = 100,
        *, selector: str | None = None, ref: int | None = None, snapshot_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/scroll", {
            "direction": direction, "pixels": pixels, "wait_ms": wait_ms,
            **{key: value for key, value in {"selector": selector, "ref": ref, "snapshot_id": snapshot_id}.items()
               if value is not None},
        })

    async def browser_wait(
        self, condition: str, value: str | None = None, timeout_ms: int = 5000, milliseconds: int = 400,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/wait", {
            "condition": condition, "value": value, "timeout_ms": timeout_ms, "milliseconds": milliseconds,
        })

    async def browser_run(self, steps: list[dict[str, Any]], timeout_ms: int = 60000, action_budget: int = 20) -> ToolResult:
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/run", {
            "steps": steps, "timeout_ms": timeout_ms, "action_budget": action_budget,
        })

    async def browser_observe(
        self, *, scope: Literal["viewport", "page"] = "viewport", text: bool = False,
        max_elements: int = 30, max_chars: int = 2400,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/observe", {
            "scope": scope, "text": text, "max_elements": max_elements, "max_chars": max_chars,
        })

    async def browser_session(
        self, action: Literal["start", "stop", "restart", "health"] = "start",
    ) -> ToolResult:
        """Control the sandbox's selected persistent browser scope."""
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/session", {"action": action})

    async def browser_open(
        self, url: str, *, new_tab: bool = False,
        wait_for: Literal["load", "domcontentloaded", "networkidle", "commit"] = "load",
    ) -> ToolResult:
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/open", {
            "url": url, "new_tab": new_tab, "wait_for": wait_for,
        })

    async def browser_tabs(
        self, action: Literal["list", "new", "switch", "close", "back", "forward", "reload"] = "list",
        *, tab: str | None = None, url: str | None = None,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {"action": action}
        if tab is not None:
            payload["tab"] = tab
        if url is not None:
            payload["url"] = url
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/tabs", payload)

    async def browser_navigate(self, url: str, wait_for: str = "load") -> ToolResult:
        """Navigate the browser to a URL."""
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/navigate", {
            "url": url,
            "wait_for": wait_for,
        })

    async def browser_get_content(
        self, mode: str = "a11y", selector: str | None = None
    ) -> ToolResult:
        """Get page content (accessibility tree, text, or HTML)."""
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {"mode": mode}
        if selector:
            payload["selector"] = selector
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/get_content", payload)

    async def browser_click(
        self,
        selector: str | None = None,
        text: str | None = None,
        role: str | None = None,
        *, ref: int | None = None, snapshot_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        """Click an element on the page."""
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {}
        if selector:
            payload["selector"] = selector
        if text:
            payload["text"] = text
        if role:
            payload["role"] = role
        if ref is not None:
            payload["ref"] = ref
        if snapshot_id is not None:
            payload["snapshot_id"] = snapshot_id
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/click", payload)

    async def browser_type(
        self,
        selector: str | None = None,
        text: str = "",
        clear_first: bool = True,
        press_enter: bool = False,
        *, ref: int | None = None, snapshot_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        """Type text into an input element."""
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "text": text,
            "clear_first": clear_first,
            "press_enter": press_enter,
        }
        if selector is not None:
            payload["selector"] = selector
        if ref is not None:
            payload["ref"] = ref
        if snapshot_id is not None:
            payload["snapshot_id"] = snapshot_id
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/type", payload)

    async def browser_screenshot(
        self, full_page: bool = False, selector: str | None = None, *, path: str | None = None, marks: bool = False,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        """Take a screenshot of the browser page."""
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {"full_page": full_page}
        if marks:
            payload["marks"] = True
        if selector:
            payload["selector"] = selector
        if path is not None:
            payload["path"] = path
        return await self._post("/tools/browser/screenshot", payload)

    # ── Health ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def health_check(self) -> bool:
        try:
            resp = await self._client.get("/health")
            return resp.status_code == 200
        except httpx.HTTPError:
            return False

    async def close(self) -> None:
        await self._client.aclose()

    # ── Internal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def _post(self, endpoint: str, payload: dict[str, Any], *, headers=None) -> ToolResult:
        """POST to the sandbox tool server and parse the response."""
        try:
            if self._browser_approval_token and endpoint in {"/tools/browser/click", "/tools/browser/type", "/tools/browser/press"}:
                headers = {**(headers or {}), "X-XBrowser-Approval": self._browser_approval_token}
                self._browser_approval_token = None
            resp = await self._client.post(endpoint, json=payload, headers=headers)
            resp.raise_for_status()
            data = resp.json()
            return ToolResult(
                success=data.get("success", False),
                output=data.get("output", ""),
                error=data.get("error"),
                exit_code=data.get("exit_code"),
                artifacts=data.get("artifacts", []),
                metadata={
                    **data.get("metadata", {}),
                    **{key: value for key, value in data.items() if key not in {
                        "success", "output", "error", "exit_code", "artifacts", "metadata",
                    }},
                },
            )
        except httpx.TimeoutException:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error="Sandbox request timed out.")
        except httpx.HTTPStatusError as exc:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False, output="",
                error=f"Sandbox returned HTTP {exc.response.status_code}.",
            )
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False, output="",
                error=f"Cannot reach sandbox: {exc}",
            )

"""Sandbox client — communicates with the FastAPI tool server inside Docker.

The sandbox client is the *only* way the agent executes actions.  All
tool implementations delegate to this client, which in turn calls
the tool server running inside the Docker container.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any

import httpx

from local_agent.tools.base import ToolResult

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    """HTTP client for the sandbox tool server."""

    def __init__(self, base_url: str = "http://localhost:9000") -> None:
        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(
            base_url=self._base_url,
            timeout=httpx.Timeout(connect=5, read=120, write=10, pool=5),
        )

    # ── Shell ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def execute_shell(
        self,
        command: str,
        working_dir: str = "/workspace",
        timeout: int = 30,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        """Execute a shell command in the sandbox."""
        return await self._post("/tools/shell", {
            "command": command,
            "working_dir": working_dir,
            "timeout": timeout,
        })

    # ── Files ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def read_file(self, path: str, *, max_lines: int = 200) -> ToolResult:

            "path": path,

    # ── Internal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def _post(self, endpoint: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        """POST to the sandbox tool server and parse the response."""
        try:
            resp = await self._client.post(endpoint, json=payload)
            resp.raise_for_status()
            data = resp.json()
            return ToolResult(
                success=data.get("success", False),
                output=data.get("output", ""),
                error=data.get("error"),
                exit_code=data.get("exit_code"),
                artifacts=data.get("artifacts", []),
            )
        except httpx.TimeoutException:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error="Sandbox request timed out.")
        except httpx.HTTPStatusError as exc:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False, output="",
                error=f"Sandbox returned HTTP {exc.response.status_code}.",
            )
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False, output="",
                error=f"Cannot reach sandbox: {exc}",
            )

"""Security: permission tiers, budget enforcement, and safety controls."""


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\security\budget.py
================================================================================

"""Budget enforcement — caps on steps, wall time, and repeated actions.

The budget tracker is injected into the agent loop and queried before
every action.  When a limit is exceeded the agent must stop or ask the
human for permission to continue.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field


class BudgetExhaustedError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when any budget limit is reached."""

    def __init__(self, reason: str) -> None:
        self.reason = reason
        super().__init__(reason)


@dataclass
class BudgetTracker:
    """Tracks step count, wall time, and repeated-action frequency."""

    max_steps: int = 50
    max_wall_time_seconds: int = 600
    max_repeated_actions: int = 3

    # ── Internal state (mutable) ─────────────────────────────────────────
    steps: int = field(default=0, init=False)
    start_time: float = field(default=0.0, init=False)
    _action_counts: Counter = field(default_factory=Counter, init=False)

    def start(self) -> None:
        """Mark the beginning of a run."""
        self.steps = 0
        self.start_time = time.monotonic()
        self._action_counts.clear()

    def record_step(self, action_key: str, *, cost: int = 1) -> None:
        """Record that one step was taken.

        Parameters
        ----------
        action_key:
            A string that uniquely identifies the action (e.g.
            ``"shell_exec:ls -la"``).  Used to detect loops.
        """
        if cost < 0:
            raise ValueError("Action cost must not be negative")
        self.steps += cost
        self._action_counts[action_key] += 1

    def check_capacity(self, cost: int) -> None:
        self.check()
        if cost < 1 or cost > self.remaining_steps:
            raise BudgetExhaustedError(f"Action allowance exceeded: requested {cost}, remaining {self.remaining_steps}.")

    @property
    def remaining_seconds(self) -> float:
        return max(0.0, self.max_wall_time_seconds - self.elapsed_seconds)

    # ── Checks ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def check(self, action_key: str | None = None) -> None:
        """Raise :class:`BudgetExhaustedError` if any limit is reached."""
        if self.steps >= self.max_steps:
            raise BudgetExhaustedError(f"Step limit reached ({self.max_steps} steps).")

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - self.start_time
        if elapsed >= self.max_wall_time_seconds:
            raise BudgetExhaustedError(
                f"Wall-time limit reached ({self.max_wall_time_seconds}s, "
                f"elapsed {elapsed:.0f}s)."
            )

        if action_key is not None and self._action_counts[action_key] >= self.max_repeated_actions:
            raise BudgetExhaustedError(
                f"Repeated-action limit reached: '{action_key}' attempted "
                f"{self._action_counts[action_key]} times."
            )

    @property
    def elapsed_seconds(self) -> float:
        return time.monotonic() - self.start_time

    @property
    def remaining_steps(self) -> int:
        return max(0, self.max_steps - self.steps)

    def summary(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        return {
            "steps": self.steps,
            "max_steps": self.max_steps,
            "elapsed_s": round(self.elapsed_seconds, 1),
            "max_time_s": self.max_wall_time_seconds,
            "top_actions": self._action_counts.most_common(5),
        }

FILE: local_agent\security\paths.py
