"""Bounded and auditable SearXNG search integration for Module 1."""

from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
import json
import os
import socket
import uuid
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
from urllib.parse import urlencode, urlparse
from urllib.request import HTTPRedirectHandler, Request, build_opener


class SearchMode(str, Enum):
    OFF = "off"
    ASK = "ask"
    ON = "on"


class SearchUnavailableError(PermissionError):
    """Raised when search has not been enabled by the user."""


class SearchPolicyError(ValueError):
    """Raised when input or a configured endpoint violates policy."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchPolicy:
    max_query_length: int = 400
    max_results: int = 10
    timeout_seconds: float = 12.0
    allowed_endpoint_hosts: tuple[str, ...] = ("localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchRequest:
    query: str
    max_results: int = 5
    allowed_domains: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    task_id: str = "default"

    def validate(self, policy: SearchPolicy) -> None:
        if not self.query or not self.query.strip():
            raise SearchPolicyError("Search query must not be empty.")
        if len(self.query) > policy.max_query_length:
            raise SearchPolicyError(f"Search query exceeds {policy.max_query_length} characters.")
        if not 1 <= self.max_results <= policy.max_results:
            raise SearchPolicyError(f"max_results must be between 1 and {policy.max_results}.")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResult:
    source_id: str
    title: str
    url: str
    snippet: str
    provider_rank: int
    retrieved_at: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResponse:
    request_id: str
    query: str
    results: tuple[SearchResult, ...]
    retrieved_at: str


def utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


def user_has_enabled_search(mode: SearchMode, approved_for_task: bool) -> bool:
    if mode is SearchMode.ON:
        return True
    if mode is SearchMode.ASK:
        return approved_for_task
    return False


def web_search_tool_definition(mode: SearchMode, approved_for_task: bool) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return no tool at all when the user has not granted network search."""
    if not user_has_enabled_search(mode, approved_for_task):
        return []
    return [{
        "type": "function",
        "function": {
            "name": "web_search",
            "description": "Search public web sources and return cited result metadata. It cannot log in, download, upload, or submit forms.",
            "parameters": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "query": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 400},
                    "max_results": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 10},
                },
                "required": ["query"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
        },
    }]


class NoRedirect(HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):  # type: ignore[override]
        return None


class SearxNGClient:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str | None = None, policy: SearchPolicy | None = None):
        self.base_url = base_url or os.environ.get("SEARXNG_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:8080")
        self.policy = policy or SearchPolicy()
        self._validate_endpoint()

    def _validate_endpoint(self) -> None:
        parsed = urlparse(self.base_url)
        if parsed.scheme != "http" or not parsed.hostname:
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG endpoint must be a local http URL.")
        host = parsed.hostname.lower()
        if host not in self.policy.allowed_endpoint_hosts:
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG endpoint host is not approved.")
        try:
            addresses = {info[4][0] for info in socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)}
        except socket.gaierror as exc:
            raise SearchPolicyError(f"Cannot resolve SearXNG endpoint: {host}") from exc
        if not all(ipaddress.ip_address(address).is_loopback for address in addresses):
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG endpoint must resolve to a loopback address.")

    def search(self, request: SearchRequest) -> SearchResponse:
        request.validate(self.policy)
        params = {"q": request.query.strip(), "format": "json", "safesearch": "1"}
        if request.allowed_domains:
            params["domains"] = ",".join(request.allowed_domains)
        url = f"{self.base_url.rstrip('/')}/search?{urlencode(params)}"
        payload = self._fetch_json(url)
        retrieved_at = utc_now()
        raw_results = payload.get("results", [])
        if not isinstance(raw_results, list):
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG returned an invalid results payload.")
        results: list[SearchResult] = []
        for rank, item in enumerate(raw_results[: request.max_results], start=1):
            if not isinstance(item, dict):
                continue
            result_url = str(item.get("url", "")).strip()
            if not result_url.startswith(("https://", "http://")):
                continue
            results.append(SearchResult(
                source_id=f"src_{rank:03d}",
                title=str(item.get("title", "Untitled source")).strip(),
                url=result_url,
                snippet=str(item.get("content", "")).strip(),
                provider_rank=rank,
                retrieved_at=retrieved_at,
            ))
        return SearchResponse(str(uuid.uuid4()), request.query.strip(), tuple(results), retrieved_at)

    def _fetch_json(self, url: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        req = Request(url, headers={"Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": "LocalAgentWebSearch/0.1"})
        opener = build_opener(NoRedirect())
        try:
            with opener.open(req, timeout=self.policy.timeout_seconds) as response:
                if response.status != 200:
                    raise SearchPolicyError(f"SearXNG returned HTTP {response.status}.")
                raw = response.read(2_000_000)
        except HTTPError as exc:
            if 300 <= exc.code < 400:
                raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG endpoint redirected; redirects are not allowed.") from exc
            raise SearchPolicyError(f"SearXNG returned HTTP {exc.code}.") from exc
        except URLError as exc:
            raise SearchPolicyError("Cannot reach the local SearXNG service.") from exc
        try:
            payload = json.loads(raw)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG did not return valid JSON.") from exc
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG returned an invalid JSON object.")
        return payload


class TraceStore:
    def __init__(self, directory: Path | str = "traces"):
        self.directory = Path(directory)

    def record(self, task_id: str, response: SearchResponse) -> Path:
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        safe_task_id = "".join(char if char.isalnum() or char in "-_" else "_" for char in task_id)[:80] or "default"
        target = self.directory / f"{safe_task_id}.jsonl"
        event = {
            "event_type": "web_search",
            "recorded_at": utc_now(),
            "response": {
                "request_id": response.request_id,
                "query": response.query,
                "retrieved_at": response.retrieved_at,
                "results": [asdict(result) for result in response.results],
            },
        }
        with target.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            stream.write(json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
        return target


class WebSearchService:
    def __init__(self, client: SearxNGClient, trace_store: TraceStore):
        self.client = client
        self.trace_store = trace_store

    def execute(self, request: SearchRequest, mode: SearchMode, approved_for_task: bool) -> SearchResponse:
        if not user_has_enabled_search(mode, approved_for_task):
            raise SearchUnavailableError("Web search is disabled. Enable it or approve this task before searching.")
        response = self.client.search(request)
        self.trace_store.record(request.task_id, response)
        return response



================================================================================
FILE: probe_model.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe why the executor model returns no tool call.

Sends the agent's REAL system prompt + REAL tool schemas to Ollama in a few variants
(context size x thinking on/off x short/long task) and prints what comes back.

Run from the project root (Ollama running, no sandbox needed):
    python probe_model.py                       # uses the executor model from config.py
    python probe_model.py --model qwen3:8b      # try another model
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import httpx

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from local_agent.config import AgentConfig  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.core.loop import EXECUTOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionPolicy  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.browser import (  # noqa: E402
    BrowserClickTool, BrowserGetContentTool, BrowserNavigateTool, BrowserScreenshotTool, BrowserTypeTool,
)
from local_agent.tools.file_ops import ListFilesTool, ReadFileTool, WriteFileTool  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.python_exec import PythonExecTool  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.rag import IngestDocumentTool, QueryDocumentsTool  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.registry import ToolRegistry  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.shell import ShellExecTool  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.web_search import WebSearchTool  # noqa: E402

LONG_GOAL = (
    "Create a complete LAN Network Inventory Dashboard as ONE file /workspace/network_dashboard/index.html "
    "with dark UI, sidebar, three summary cards, a device table of six devices, search, All/Online/Offline "
    "filters, a clear-search button, responsive layout, embedded CSS and JavaScript. Use write_file."
)
SHORT_GOAL = "Create the file /workspace/hello.txt containing the text Hello World. Use write_file."


def build_registry(all_tools: bool) -> ToolRegistry:
    reg = ToolRegistry(PermissionPolicy())
    if all_tools:
        reg.register_many(
            ShellExecTool(), ReadFileTool(), WriteFileTool(), ListFilesTool(), PythonExecTool(),
            WebSearchTool(), BrowserNavigateTool(), BrowserGetContentTool(), BrowserClickTool(),
            BrowserTypeTool(), BrowserScreenshotTool(), IngestDocumentTool(), QueryDocumentsTool(),
        )
    else:
        reg.register(WriteFileTool())
    return reg


async def probe(client, model, goal, ctx, think, all_tools, num_predict):
    reg = build_registry(all_tools)
    system = EXECUTOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT.format(
        tool_names=", ".join(reg.names),
        state_summary=f"Goal: {goal}\nStatus: executing\nStep: 1/2\nCurrent task: {goal}",
    )
    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "stream": False,
        "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system},
                     {"role": "user", "content": f"Execute this step: {goal}"}],
        "tools": reg.get_tool_definitions(),
        "options": {"num_ctx": ctx, "temperature": 0.3, "num_predict": num_predict},
    }
    if think is not None:
        payload["think"] = think
    t0 = time.time()
    r = await client.post("/api/chat", json=payload)
    dt = time.time() - t0
    if r.status_code != 200:
        return {"error": f"HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:200]}", "secs": dt}
    d = r.json()
    m = d.get("message", {})
    return {
        "prompt_tok": d.get("prompt_eval_count"),
        "gen_tok": d.get("eval_count"),
        "done_reason": d.get("done_reason"),
        "content_chars": len(m.get("content", "") or ""),
        "thinking_chars": len(m.get("thinking", "") or ""),
        "tool_calls": [tc.get("function", {}).get("name") for tc in m.get("tool_calls", []) or []],
        "content_head": (m.get("content", "") or "")[:120].replace("\n", " "),
        "secs": dt,
    }


async def main() -> None:
    cfg = AgentConfig.from_env()
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=cfg.model_roles["executor"].model)
    ap.add_argument("--num-predict", type=int, default=3000)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    variants = [
        # label, goal, ctx, think, all_tools
        ("short task, 1 tool, ctx 8192, think off", SHORT_GOAL, 8192, False, False),
        ("short task, all tools, ctx 8192, think off", SHORT_GOAL, 8192, False, True),
        ("LONG task, all tools, ctx 4096, think default", LONG_GOAL, 4096, None, True),
        ("LONG task, all tools, ctx 4096, think off", LONG_GOAL, 4096, False, True),
        ("LONG task, all tools, ctx 8192, think default", LONG_GOAL, 8192, None, True),
        ("LONG task, all tools, ctx 8192, think off", LONG_GOAL, 8192, False, True),
    ]
    print(f"model = {args.model}   (num_predict cap {args.num_predict})\n")
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=cfg.ollama_base_url, timeout=600) as client:
        for label, goal, ctx, think, all_tools in variants:
            print(f"--- {label}")
            res = await probe(client, args.model, goal, ctx, think, all_tools, args.num_predict)
            print("   ", json.dumps(res))
    print(
        "\nHow to read this:\n"
        " * prompt_tok close to num_ctx  -> input is being truncated: raise context_length.\n"
        " * done_reason=length + no tool_calls -> ran out of tokens (thinking or a long file body).\n"
        " * thinking_chars large with think default -> thinking is eating the budget: send think=false.\n"
        " * short task fails too -> the model/template isn't producing tool_calls: try another model tag.\n"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

================================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================================

# XALGV — Local Autonomous Computer-Using Agent

A local-first, sandboxed, multimodal computer-using agent with dynamic planning, tool execution, browser automation, document intelligence, vision, translation, and custom-model integration.

**One-sentence description:** A local, sandboxed agent that observes a computer environment, plans, acts through tools, verifies results, and adapts — running entirely on your laptop with open-source models served through Ollama.

## Architecture

XBrowser includes persistent sessions and snapshot-scoped element targeting. See
[session architecture and setup](docs/xbrowser-foundation.md) and
[observations, references, and current limits](docs/xbrowser-observations.md).
The [execution architecture](docs/xbrowser-architecture.md) and
[bounded workflow API](docs/xbrowser-execution.md) cover the nine actions, macros,
agent progress and budgets. See the [v1 completion and validation report](docs/xbrowser-validation.md)
for the implemented scope, recorded results, and operating limits.
Trusted completion criteria, network controls, approvals, token budgeting, and
visual fallback are documented in [XBrowser security and verification](docs/xbrowser-security.md).

```
User Goal → Planner → [Observe → Decide → Permission Check → Act → Verify] → Done?
                                                                      ↑              |
                                                                      +-- Adapt/Re-plan ←-+

Host Machine:                          Docker Sandbox:
├── Ollama (GPU, model serving)        ├── FastAPI Tool Server (port 9000)
├── Agent Core (Python)                ├── Xvfb + Openbox (headless GUI)
├── Model Manager (VRAM scheduler)     ├── Firefox/Chromium (browser)
├── Memory (SQLite)                    ├── noVNC (port 6080, watch live)
└── connects over HTTP ───────────────→└── /workspace volume
```

## Quick Start

### 1. Prerequisites

- Python 3.11+
- Docker & Docker Compose
- [Ollama](https://ollama.ai) with `qwen3.5:9b-q4_K_M` (or another model selected with `--model`)

### 2. Install

```bash
pip install -e ".[dev]"
```

### 3. Start Services

```bash
# Start SearXNG and the sandbox
docker compose up -d

# Pull the default model
ollama pull qwen3.5:9b-q4_K_M
```

### 4. Check Health

```bash
python -m local_agent.main health
```

### 5. Run the Agent

```bash
python -m local_agent.main run --model qwen3.5:9b-q4_K_M "Create a Python hello-world project with tests"
```

Pass any installed Ollama model tag with `--model`. This overrides the agent's
planner, executor, router, vision, and translation roles for that run; the
configured embedding model is left unchanged.

### Local prompt and screenshot UI

Start the browser sandbox and Ollama, then run:

```powershell
.\venv\Scripts\python.exe -m local_agent.main ui
```

Open [http://127.0.0.1:7860](http://127.0.0.1:7860). Choose an installed Ollama
model, enter a task, and optionally attach a PNG or JPEG screenshot (up to 8 MiB)
to ask a question about its visible text or content. The model can also call the
`image_question` tool on images saved under `workspace/uploads/`. Runs and their
tool results appear on the page. The UI binds to localhost by default.

### 6. Run Evaluation

```bash
python -m local_agent.main eval
python -m local_agent.main eval --category shell
```

### 7. Web Search

```bash
python -m local_agent.main search "local agent tool calling" --mode on
```

### 8. Watch the Agent Live

Open http://localhost:6080 in your browser to see the sandbox desktop via noVNC.

## Project Structure

```
├── local_agent/                # Main package
│   ├── config.py              # Configuration system
│   ├── models.py              # Ollama model manager with VRAM scheduling
│   ├── main.py                # CLI entry point
│   ├── web_search.py          # SearXNG search integration
│   ├── core/                  # Agent core
│   │   ├── loop.py            # ReAct control loop
│   │   ├── planner.py         # LLM-based plan generation
│   │   ├── verifier.py        # Post-action verification
│   │   └── state.py           # Mutable agent state
│   ├── tools/                 # Tool system
│   │   ├── base.py            # Abstract Tool base class
│   │   ├── registry.py        # Tool registry with permission gating
│   │   ├── shell.py           # Shell execution tool
│   │   ├── file_ops.py        # File read/write/list tools
│   │   └── python_exec.py     # Python code execution tool
│   ├── memory/                # Memory subsystem
│   │   ├── store.py           # SQLite persistent store
│   │   ├── working.py         # Rolling context window
│   │   └── checkpoint.py      # Checkpoint and resume
│   ├── security/              # Security & safety
│   │   ├── permissions.py     # 5-tier permission system
│   │   └── budget.py          # Step/time/repeat budget enforcement
│   └── sandbox/               # Sandbox communication
│       └── client.py          # HTTP client for the tool server
├── sandbox/                   # Docker sandbox definition
│   ├── Dockerfile             # Debian-based Linux workstation
│   ├── tool_server.py         # FastAPI tool server
│   └── entrypoint.sh          # Starts Xvfb, Openbox, noVNC, tool server
├── eval/                      # Evaluation harness
│   ├── harness.py             # Task runner and reporting
│   └── tasks/                 # Task suites (JSON)
│       ├── shell_tasks.json   # 5 shell/filesystem tasks
│       ├── file_tasks.json    # 5 file operation tasks
│       └── python_tasks.json  # 5 Python coding tasks
├── tests/                     # Unit tests
├── searxng/                   # SearXNG config
├── compose.yaml               # Docker Compose for sandbox + SearXNG
└── pyproject.toml             # Project metadata
```

## Design Principles

| Principle | Meaning |
|-----------|---------|
| Dynamic decisions, fixed scaffolding | The loop, tool schemas, permissions, and budgets are fixed and tested |
| Observe before and after acting | Every action is followed by verification |
| Structure first, pixels second | DOM/accessibility trees before screenshots |
| External verification | Check outcomes with code, not LLM judgement |
| Sandbox by default | Agent never runs on the host |
| Local-first | Inference, memory, documents stay local |
| One model at a time | VRAM-aware scheduler swaps models |
| Measure everything | Eval harness runs after every change |

## Permission Tiers

| Tier | Level | Auto-approved | Examples |
|------|-------|---------------|----------|
| READ_ONLY | 0 | ✓ | Read files, list dirs, screenshots |
| WORKSPACE_WRITE | 1 | ✓ | Write/delete in /workspace |
| NETWORK | 2 | ✗ | Web search, fetch pages |
| SYSTEM | 3 | ✗ | Install packages, change config |
| DESTRUCTIVE | 4 | ✗ (always needs human) | rm -rf, drop tables |

## Running Tests

```bash
python -m pytest tests/ -v
```

## Roadmap

- [x] **Phase 0:** Environment, Ollama, tool-calling hello world
- [x] **Phase 1:** Core loop, sandbox, shell/file/Python tools, eval harness
- [ ] **Phase 2:** Browser automation (Playwright), SearXNG integration, RAG
- [ ] **Phase 3:** GUI desktop (Xvfb), VLM, LibreOffice
- [ ] **Phase 4:** Translation, speech, image generation, VRAM scheduler
- [ ] **Phase 5:** Skill library, memory, fine-tuning, red teaming, UI

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run health check, unit tests and eval suites; store everything under results/.

Usage (from the project root):
    python run_checks.py                       # everything
    python run_checks.py --tag qwen3-8b        # label the run (shows up in history.csv)
    python run_checks.py --categories shell file
    python run_checks.py --skip-eval           # unit tests + health only
    python run_checks.py --force               # continue even if health fails

Model selection comes from XALGV_* environment variables (see config.py),
so set them before running this script.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CATEGORIES = ["shell", "file", "python", "browser", "combined"]
PY = sys.executable


def run(cmd: list[str], out_file: Path, timeout: int | None = None) -> int:
    """Run a command, stream output to console and save it to out_file."""
    print(f"\n$ {' '.join(cmd)}")
    with out_file.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        try:
            proc = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd, cwd=ROOT, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
            )
        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
            msg = f"command not found: {exc}\n"
            print(msg)
            fh.write(msg)
            return 127
        start = time.time()
        assert proc.stdout is not None
        for line in proc.stdout:
            sys.stdout.write(line)
            fh.write(line)
            if timeout and time.time() - start > timeout:
                proc.kill()
                fh.write(f"\n[run_checks] killed after {timeout}s timeout\n")
                return 124
        return proc.wait()


def capture(cmd: list[str]) -> str:
    try:
        return subprocess.run(
            cmd, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30,
            encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
        ).stdout.strip()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return f"(unavailable: {exc})"


class VramSampler(threading.Thread):
    """Samples GPU memory used (MiB) via nvidia-smi while a command runs."""

    def __init__(self, interval: float = 2.0) -> None:
        super().__init__(daemon=True)
        self.interval = interval
        self.peak = 0
        self._halt = threading.Event()
        self.available = shutil.which("nvidia-smi") is not None

    def run(self) -> None:
        while self.available and not self._halt.is_set():
            out = capture(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=memory.used", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"])
            try:
                self.peak = max(self.peak, int(out.splitlines()[0].strip()))
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                pass
            self._halt.wait(self.interval)

    def stop(self) -> int:
        self._halt.set()
        return self.peak


def model_roles() -> dict:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
    try:
        from local_agent.config import AgentConfig

        cfg = AgentConfig.from_env()
        return {
            role: {
                "model": rc.model, "num_ctx": rc.context_length,
                "temperature": rc.temperature, "thinking": rc.thinking,
            }
            for role, rc in cfg.model_roles.items()
        }
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return {"error": str(exc)}


def summarize(report_path: Path) -> dict:
    data = json.loads(report_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    results = data.get("results", [])
    steps = [r.get("steps_taken", 0) for r in results]
    failures = []
    for r in results:
        if r.get("passed"):
            continue
        reason = r.get("error")
        if not reason:
            bad = [c for c in r.get("checker_results", []) if not c.get("passed", False)]
            reason = "; ".join(str(c.get("description") or c.get("path") or c.get("error") or c.get("type")) for c in bad)
        failures.append({"task_id": r.get("task_id"), "steps": r.get("steps_taken"), "reason": reason or "unknown"})
    return {
        "total": data.get("total_tasks", len(results)),
        "passed": data.get("passed", 0),
        "failed": data.get("failed", 0),
        "errors": data.get("errors", 0),
        "success_rate": data.get("success_rate", 0.0),
        "wall_time_s": data.get("wall_time_seconds", 0.0),
        "avg_steps": round(sum(steps) / len(steps), 1) if steps else 0,
        "failures": failures,
    }


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="", help="label for this run, e.g. the model name")
    ap.add_argument("--categories", nargs="*", default=CATEGORIES)
    ap.add_argument("--skip-unit", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--skip-eval", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="continue even if health check fails")
    ap.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=3600, help="per-category eval timeout (s)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    out = ROOT / "results" / (stamp + (f"_{args.tag}" if args.tag else ""))
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    print(f"Results directory: {out}")

    # 1. Environment snapshot (so results are reproducible and comparable).
    env = {
        "timestamp": stamp,
        "tag": args.tag,
        "python": sys.version.split()[0],
        "git_commit": capture(["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"]),
        "git_dirty": bool(capture(["git", "status", "--porcelain"])),
        "model_roles": model_roles(),
        "ollama_list": capture(["ollama", "list"]),
        "gpu": capture(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=name,memory.total,driver_version", "--format=csv,noheader"]),
        "docker_ps": capture(["docker", "compose", "ps", "--format", "table"]),
    }
    (out / "environment.json").write_text(json.dumps(env, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    summary: dict = {"environment": env, "unit_tests": None, "health": None, "eval": {}}

    # 2. Health (Ollama + sandbox reachable).
    rc = run([PY, "-m", "local_agent.main", "health"], out / "health.txt")
    summary["health"] = "ok" if rc == 0 else "FAILED"
    if rc != 0 and not args.force and not args.skip_eval:
        print("\nHealth check failed: start Ollama and `docker compose up -d`, or use --force.")
        (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        return 2

    # 3. Unit tests.
    if not args.skip_unit:
        rc = run([PY, "-m", "pytest", "tests", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider"], out / "unit_tests.txt")
        tail = (out / "unit_tests.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip().splitlines()
        summary["unit_tests"] = {"exit_code": rc, "result": tail[-1] if tail else ""}

    # 4. Eval suites, one category at a time, with peak VRAM sampling.
    if not args.skip_eval:
        report_src = ROOT / "traces" / "eval_report.json"
        for cat in args.categories:
            if report_src.exists():
                report_src.unlink()  # avoid copying a stale report
            sampler = VramSampler()
            sampler.start()
            t0 = time.time()
            rc = run([PY, "-m", "local_agent.main", "-v", "eval", "--category", cat],
                     out / f"eval_{cat}.log", timeout=args.timeout)
            peak = sampler.stop()
            if report_src.exists():
                shutil.copy(report_src, out / f"eval_{cat}.json")
                s = summarize(out / f"eval_{cat}.json")
            else:
                s = {"total": 0, "passed": 0, "failed": 0, "errors": 0, "success_rate": 0.0,
                     "wall_time_s": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "avg_steps": 0,
                     "failures": [{"task_id": "-", "steps": 0, "reason": f"no report produced (exit {rc})"}]}
            s["peak_vram_mib"] = peak or None
            s["exit_code"] = rc
            summary["eval"][cat] = s

        db = ROOT / "data" / "agent.db"
        if db.exists():
            shutil.copy(db, out / "agent.db")  # run + step traces

    # 5. Write summary.json, summary.md and append to history.csv.
    (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    lines = [f"# Run {stamp} {args.tag}".strip(), ""]
    lines.append(f"- Health: {summary['health']}")
    if summary["unit_tests"]:
        lines.append(f"- Unit tests: {summary['unit_tests']['result']}")
    lines.append(f"- Models: " + ", ".join(
        f"{r}={v['model']}" for r, v in env["model_roles"].items() if isinstance(v, dict) and r in ("planner", "executor")))
    lines += ["", "| Category | Passed | Rate | Avg steps | Time (s) | Peak VRAM (MiB) |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for cat, s in summary["eval"].items():
        lines.append(f"| {cat} | {s['passed']}/{s['total']} | {s['success_rate']:.0%} | {s['avg_steps']} | "
                     f"{s['wall_time_s']} | {s['peak_vram_mib'] or 'n/a'} |")
    fails = [(c, f) for c, s in summary["eval"].items() for f in s["failures"]]
    if fails:
        lines += ["", "## Failures", ""]
        lines += [f"- [{c}] {f['task_id']} ({f['steps']} steps): {f['reason']}" for c, f in fails]
    (out / "summary.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    hist = ROOT / "results" / "history.csv"
    new = not hist.exists()
    with hist.open("a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        w = csv.writer(fh)
        if new:
            w.writerow(["timestamp", "tag", "git", "executor_model", "category", "passed", "total",
                        "success_rate", "avg_steps", "wall_time_s", "peak_vram_mib"])
        ex = env["model_roles"].get("executor", {}).get("model", "?") if isinstance(env["model_roles"], dict) else "?"
        for cat, s in summary["eval"].items():
            w.writerow([stamp, args.tag, env["git_commit"], ex, cat, s["passed"], s["total"],
                        s["success_rate"], s["avg_steps"], s["wall_time_s"], s["peak_vram_mib"]])

    print("\n" + "\n".join(lines))
    print(f"\nSaved to {out}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

FILE: sandbox\browser_server.py

"""Thin browser API with legacy compatibility over the XBrowser session."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
import secrets
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import APIRouter, Header, HTTPException
from fastapi import Request as Connection
from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError
from pydantic import TypeAdapter

if __package__:  # Package imports in tests; flat imports in the Docker image.
    from .xbrowser import macros
    from .xbrowser.controller import execute
    from .xbrowser.controller import failure as action_failure
    from .xbrowser.errors import BrowserError
    from .xbrowser.images import image_data
    from .xbrowser.safety import BrowserPolicy
    from .xbrowser.schemas import (
        ApprovalRequest,
        BrowserResponse,
        BrowserStep,
        ClickRequest,
        ImageRequest,
        ObserveRequest,
        OpenRequest,
        PressRequest,
        Request,
        RunRequest,
        ScreenshotRequest,
        ScrollRequest,
        SessionRequest,
        TabsRequest,
        TypeRequest,
        VerificationRequest,
        WaitRequest,
    )
    from .xbrowser.session import BrowserSession
    from .xbrowser.verification import verify as verify_task
else:
    from xbrowser import macros
    from xbrowser.controller import execute
    from xbrowser.controller import failure as action_failure
    from xbrowser.errors import BrowserError
    from xbrowser.images import image_data
    from xbrowser.safety import BrowserPolicy
    from xbrowser.schemas import (
        ApprovalRequest,
        BrowserResponse,
        BrowserStep,
        ClickRequest,
        ImageRequest,
        ObserveRequest,
        OpenRequest,
        PressRequest,
        Request,
        RunRequest,
        ScreenshotRequest,
        ScrollRequest,
        SessionRequest,
        TabsRequest,
        TypeRequest,
        VerificationRequest,
        WaitRequest,
    )
    from xbrowser.session import BrowserSession
    from xbrowser.verification import verify as verify_task

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
session = BrowserSession(
    os.environ.get("XBROWSER_WORKSPACE", "/workspace"),
    scope=os.environ.get("XBROWSER_SCOPE", "default"),
    headless=os.environ.get("XBROWSER_HEADLESS", "0") == "1",
)

session.policy = BrowserPolicy.from_env()


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(_app):
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        await session.control("stop")


router = APIRouter(prefix="/tools/browser", tags=["browser"], lifespan=lifespan)


class ContentRequest(Request):
    mode: str = "a11y"
    selector: str | None = None


def failure(action: str, exc: Exception):
    logger.warning("Browser %s failed: %s", action, exc)
    return action_failure(session, action, exc)


@router.post("/session", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def session_control(req: SessionRequest):
    try:
        return await session.control(req.action)
    except (BrowserError, PlaywrightError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        return failure(f"session_{req.action}", exc)


@router.post("/open", response_model=BrowserResponse)
@router.post("/navigate", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def navigate(req: OpenRequest):
    return await execute(session, "open", req)


@router.post("/tabs", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def tabs(req: TabsRequest):
    return await execute(session, "tabs", req)


@router.post("/get_content", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def get_content(req: ContentRequest):
    try:
        session.policy.check("observe")
        async with session.operation() as page:
            target = page.locator(req.selector or "body")
            if req.mode == "a11y":
                output = await target.aria_snapshot(timeout=5000)
            elif req.mode == "text":
                output = await target.inner_text(timeout=5000)
            elif req.mode == "html":
                if req.selector:
                    output = await target.inner_html(timeout=5000)
                else:
                    output = await page.evaluate("""() => {
                        const clone = document.body.cloneNode(true);
                        clone.querySelectorAll('script, style, noscript, svg, img').forEach(el => el.remove());
                        return clone.innerHTML.replace(/\\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                    }""")
            else:
                raise BrowserError("INVALID_MODE", f"Unknown content mode: {req.mode}")
            result = await session.describe("get_content")
            result.output = output[:20000]
            return result
    except (BrowserError, PlaywrightError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        return failure("get_content", exc)


@router.post("/observe", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def observe(req: ObserveRequest):
    return await execute(session, "observe", req)


@router.post("/click", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def click(req: ClickRequest, x_xbrowser_approval: str | None = Header(default=None)):
    return await execute(session, "click", req, approval_token=x_xbrowser_approval)


@router.post("/type", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def type_text(req: TypeRequest, x_xbrowser_approval: str | None = Header(default=None)):
    return await execute(session, "type", req, approval_token=x_xbrowser_approval)


@router.post("/screenshot", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def browser_screenshot(req: ScreenshotRequest):
    return await execute(session, "screenshot", req)


@router.post("/scroll", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def scroll(req: ScrollRequest):
    return await execute(session, "scroll", req)


@router.post("/press", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def press(req: PressRequest, x_xbrowser_approval: str | None = Header(default=None)):
    return await execute(session, "press", req, approval_token=x_xbrowser_approval)


@router.post("/wait", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def wait(req: WaitRequest):
    return await execute(session, "wait", req)


@router.post("/run", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def run(req: RunRequest, connection: Connection):
    return await macros.run_connected(session, req, connection)


@router.post("/authorize", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def authorize(req: ApprovalRequest, x_xbrowser_admin: str = Header(default="")):
    secret = os.environ.get("XBROWSER_ADMIN_TOKEN", "")
    if not secret or not secrets.compare_digest(secret, x_xbrowser_admin):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Trusted approval control is disabled or unauthorized")
    try:
        step = TypeAdapter(BrowserStep).validate_python({"tool": req.tool, "args": req.args})
        async with session.operation():
            token = await session.approvals.issue(session, step.tool, step.args)
            result = await session.describe("authorize")
            result.data = {"approval_token": token, "expires_in_seconds": 120}
            return result
    except (BrowserError, PlaywrightError, ValueError) as exc:
        return failure("authorize", exc)


@router.post("/verify", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def verify(req: VerificationRequest):
    try:
        session.policy.check("observe")
        return await verify_task(session, req)
    except (BrowserError, PlaywrightError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        return failure("verify", exc)


@router.post("/image", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def image(req: ImageRequest):
    try:
        session.policy.check("screenshot")
        return BrowserResponse(success=True, action="image", data=image_data(session, req.path))
    except (BrowserError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        return failure("image", exc)

================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\entrypoint.sh
================================================================================

#!/bin/bash
set -Eeuo pipefail

# A container restart preserves /tmp but gives services a new PID namespace.
display_number="${DISPLAY#:}"
display_number="${display_number%%.*}"
[[ "$display_number" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || { echo "Invalid DISPLAY" >&2; exit 1; }
if ! xwininfo -root -display "$DISPLAY" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    rm -f -- "/tmp/.X${display_number}-lock" "/tmp/.X11-unix/X${display_number}"
fi

services=()
server_pid=""
cleanup() {
    trap - EXIT TERM INT
    # Let Playwright close its persistent profile while the display is alive.
    if [[ -n "$server_pid" ]]; then
        kill "$server_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        wait "$server_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
    if ((${#services[@]})); then
        kill "${services[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true
        wait "${services[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'exit 143' TERM
trap 'exit 130' INT

wait_for() {
    local process="$1"
    shift
    for ((attempt=0; attempt<100; attempt++)); do
        if "$@" >/dev/null 2>&1; then return 0; fi
        kill -0 "$process" 2>/dev/null || { echo "Required desktop service exited" >&2; return 1; }
        sleep 0.1
    done
    echo "Desktop service readiness timed out" >&2
    return 1
}

echo "[entrypoint] Starting Xvfb on $DISPLAY..."
Xvfb "$DISPLAY" -screen 0 "$XVFB_RESOLUTION" -ac +extension GLX +render -noreset &
xvfb_pid=$!
services+=("$xvfb_pid")
wait_for "$xvfb_pid" xwininfo -root -display "$DISPLAY"

openbox &
services+=("$!")

x11vnc -display "$DISPLAY" -forever -nopw -shared -listen 127.0.0.1 -rfbport 5900 &
vnc_pid=$!
services+=("$vnc_pid")
wait_for "$vnc_pid" python3 /opt/tool_server/healthcheck.py --vnc-only

websockify --web=/usr/share/novnc/ 6080 127.0.0.1:5900 &
services+=("$!")
python3 -m uvicorn tool_server:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 9000 --app-dir /opt/tool_server --timeout-graceful-shutdown 5 &
server_pid=$!
services+=("$server_pid")

# A failed desktop service must restart the container, rather than leave a
# healthy API with an unusable display/VNC connection.
set +e
wait -n "${services[@]}"
status=$?
set -e
echo "[entrypoint] Required service exited (status $status); restarting stack" >&2
exit 1

"""Health includes the live desktop and VNC backend, not just FastAPI."""

import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import urllib.request


def main():
    try:
        with socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", 5900), timeout=1) as connection:
            if not connection.recv(12).startswith(b"RFB "):
                return 1
        if "--vnc-only" in sys.argv:
            return 0
        subprocess.run(["xwininfo", "-root"], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=1, check=True)
        for url in ("http://127.0.0.1:9000/health", "http://127.0.0.1:6080/vnc.html"):
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=1) as response:
                if response.status != 200:
                    return 1
        return 0
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\tool_server.py
================================================================================


"""FastAPI tool server — runs inside the Docker sandbox.

This server exposes shell, file, and screenshot tools over HTTP.
The agent core (on the host) calls these endpoints to execute actions
inside the isolated environment.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import base64
import os
import secrets
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi import FastAPI, Header, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel

app = FastAPI(title="XALGV Sandbox Tool Server", version="0.1.0")

# ── Register browser automation routes ───────────────────────────────────
if __package__:
    from .browser_server import router as browser_router
else:
    from browser_server import router as browser_router
app.include_router(browser_router)

WORKSPACE = Path("/workspace")


def protected_profile(path: Path) -> bool:
    return any(part.casefold() == ".xbrowser" for part in (*path.parts, *path.resolve().parts))


def workspace_path(path: Path) -> Path:
    resolved = path.resolve()
    if not resolved.is_relative_to(WORKSPACE.resolve()) or protected_profile(path):
        raise PermissionError("File access must stay inside the workspace and outside browser profiles")
    return resolved


# ── Request / Response models ────────────────────────────────────────────

class ToolResponse(BaseModel):
    success: bool
    output: str = ""
    error: str | None = None
    exit_code: int | None = None
    artifacts: list[str] = []


class ShellRequest(BaseModel):
    command: str
    working_dir: str = "/workspace"
    timeout: int = 30


class ReadFileRequest(BaseModel):
    path: str
    max_lines: int = 200


class WriteFileRequest(BaseModel):
    path: str
    content: str
    append: bool = False


class ListFilesRequest(BaseModel):
    path: str = "/workspace"


# ── Health ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.get("/health")
async def health() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"status": "ok"}


# ── Shell execution ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.post("/tools/shell", response_model=ToolResponse)
async def shell_exec(req: ShellRequest, x_sandbox_admin: str = Header(default="")) -> ToolResponse:
    """Execute a shell command and return stdout/stderr."""
    expected = os.environ.get("SANDBOX_ADMIN_TOKEN", "")
    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(expected, x_sandbox_admin):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Arbitrary execution requires trusted sandbox administration")
    try:
        result = await asyncio.wait_for(
            asyncio.create_subprocess_shell(
                req.command,
                cwd=req.working_dir,
                stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                env={**os.environ, "TERM": "dumb"},
            ),
            timeout=req.timeout + 5,
        )
        stdout, stderr = await asyncio.wait_for(
            result.communicate(),
            timeout=req.timeout,
        )
        output = stdout.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        err_text = stderr.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        combined = output
        if err_text:
            combined += f"\n--- stderr ---\n{err_text}"
        return ToolResponse(
            success=result.returncode == 0,
            output=combined[:50_000],  # Cap output size.
            error=err_text[:5000] if result.returncode != 0 and err_text else None,
            exit_code=result.returncode,
        )
    except asyncio.TimeoutError:
        return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"Command timed out after {req.timeout}s.")
    except Exception as exc:
        return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"Execution error: {exc}")


# ── File operations ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.post("/tools/read_file", response_model=ToolResponse)
async def read_file(req: ReadFileRequest) -> ToolResponse:
    """Read a file and return its contents."""
    path = Path(req.path)
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = WORKSPACE / path
    try:
        path = workspace_path(path)
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        lines = text.splitlines()
        if len(lines) > req.max_lines:
            text = "\n".join(lines[: req.max_lines])
            text += f"\n\n... [{len(lines) - req.max_lines} more lines truncated]"
        return ToolResponse(success=True, output=text)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"File not found: {req.path}")
    except Exception as exc:
        return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"Read error: {exc}")


@app.post("/tools/write_file", response_model=ToolResponse)
async def write_file(req: WriteFileRequest) -> ToolResponse:
    """Write content to a file, creating parent directories."""
    path = Path(req.path)
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = WORKSPACE / path
    try:
        path = workspace_path(path)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if req.append:
            with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                fh.write(req.content)
        else:
            path.write_text(req.content, encoding="utf-8")
        verb = "Appended" if req.append else "Wrote"
        return ToolResponse(
            success=True,
            output=f"{verb} {len(req.content)} chars to {path} (file size now {path.stat().st_size} bytes)",
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"Write error: {exc}")


@app.post("/tools/list_files", response_model=ToolResponse)
async def list_files(req: ListFilesRequest) -> ToolResponse:
    """List directory contents."""
    path = Path(req.path)
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = WORKSPACE / path
    try:
        path = workspace_path(path)
        if not path.is_dir():
            return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"Not a directory: {req.path}")
        entries: list[str] = []
        for entry in sorted(path.iterdir()):
            if protected_profile(entry):
                continue
            kind = "dir" if entry.is_dir() else "file"
            size = entry.stat().st_size if entry.is_file() else 0
            entries.append(f"{kind}\t{size}\t{entry.name}")
        return ToolResponse(success=True, output="\n".join(entries))
    except Exception as exc:
        return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"List error: {exc}")


# ── Screenshot ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.post("/tools/screenshot", response_model=ToolResponse)
async def screenshot() -> ToolResponse:
    """Take a screenshot using scrot and return the file path."""
    screenshot_path = "/tmp/screenshot.png"
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["scrot", screenshot_path],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10,
            env={**os.environ, "DISPLAY": os.environ.get("DISPLAY", ":99")},
        )
        if result.returncode != 0:
            return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"scrot failed: {result.stderr}")
        return ToolResponse(
            success=True,
            output=f"Screenshot saved to {screenshot_path}",
            artifacts=[screenshot_path],
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        return ToolResponse(success=False, output="", error=f"Screenshot error: {exc}")

This server exposes shell, file, and screenshot tools over HTTP.
The agent core (on the host) calls these endpoints to execute actions
inside the isolated environment.

    from browser_server import router as browser_router
    app.include_router(browser_router)

    pass

"""XBrowser: sandbox-owned Chromium lifecycle and stable tab control."""

from .session import BrowserSession

================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\actions.py

"""Reference-aware actions with deterministic mechanical evidence."""

import asyncio

from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError

from .approvals import guard_key, guard_target
from .state import capture_state


async def target_for(session, page, request):
    if request.ref is not None:
        return await session.references.resolve(session, request.snapshot_id, request.ref)
    if request.selector:
        return page.locator(request.selector)
    if request.role:
        return page.get_by_role(request.role, name=request.text)
    return page.get_by_text(request.text, exact=False).first


async def finish(session, page, action, before, evidence):
    result = await session.describe(action, evidence=evidence)
    result.state_before = before["fingerprint"]
    try:
        after = await capture_state(page)
        result.state_after = after["fingerprint"]
        result.state_changed = result.state_before != result.state_after
        result.action_verification["url_changed"] = before["url"] != after["url"]
    except PlaywrightError:
        result.data["warning"] = "Action dispatched, but page closed or navigated before state capture; observe before proceeding"
    if action == "click" and not result.state_changed:
        result.data.setdefault("warning", "Click dispatched; no immediate page-state change detected")
    if "warning" in result.data:
        result.output += "\n" + result.data["warning"]
    return result


async def click(session, page, request):
    target = await target_for(session, page, request)
    await guard_target(session, target)
    before = await capture_state(page)
    await target.click(timeout=5000)
    return await finish(session, page, "click", before, {"dispatched": True})


async def type_text(session, page, request):
    target = await target_for(session, page, request)
    await guard_target(session, target)
    before = await capture_state(page)
    old_value = await target.evaluate("el => el.isContentEditable ? el.textContent : el.value")
    if request.clear_first:
        await target.fill(request.text, timeout=5000)
    else:
        await target.type(request.text, timeout=5000)
    expected = request.text if request.clear_first else (old_value or "") + request.text
    matches = await target.evaluate("(el, expected) => (el.isContentEditable ? el.textContent : el.value) === expected", expected)
    if request.press_enter and matches:
        await target.press("Enter", timeout=5000)
    result = await finish(session, page, "type", before, {"value_matches": matches, "submitted": request.press_enter and matches})
    if not matches:
        result.success = False
        result.error_code = "VALUE_MISMATCH"
        result.error = "Field value did not match the requested text; submission was skipped"
    return result


async def press(session, page, request):
    await guard_key(session, page, request.key)
    before = await capture_state(page)
    await page.keyboard.press(request.key)
    return await finish(session, page, "press", before, {"dispatched": True, "key": request.key})


async def wait(session, page, request):
    if request.condition == "text":
        await page.get_by_text(request.value, exact=False).first.wait_for(state="visible", timeout=request.timeout_ms)
    elif request.condition == "selector":
        await page.locator(request.value).wait_for(state="visible", timeout=request.timeout_ms)
    elif request.condition == "idle":
        await page.wait_for_load_state("networkidle", timeout=request.timeout_ms)
    else:
        await asyncio.sleep(request.milliseconds / 1000)
    return await session.describe("wait", evidence={"condition_met": True, "condition": request.condition})


SCROLL = """(el, {direction, pixels}) => {
    const node=el || document.scrollingElement;
    const before=[node.scrollLeft,node.scrollTop];
    let x=node.scrollLeft, y=node.scrollTop;
    if(direction==='top') y=0;
    if(direction==='bottom') y=node.scrollHeight;
    if(direction==='up') y-=pixels;
    if(direction==='down') y+=pixels;
    if(direction==='left') x-=pixels;
    if(direction==='right') x+=pixels;
    node.scrollTo({left:x,top:y,behavior:'instant'});
    return {before, position:[node.scrollLeft,node.scrollTop],
        moved:before[0]!==node.scrollLeft || before[1]!==node.scrollTop,
        at_top:node.scrollTop<=1, at_bottom:node.scrollTop+node.clientHeight>=node.scrollHeight-1,
        at_left:node.scrollLeft<=1, at_right:node.scrollLeft+node.clientWidth>=node.scrollWidth-1};
}"""


async def scroll(session, page, request):
    payload = {"direction": request.direction, "pixels": request.pixels}
    if request.ref is not None or request.selector:
        target = await target_for(session, page, request)
        evidence = await target.evaluate(SCROLL, payload)
    else:
        evidence = await page.evaluate(f"args => ({SCROLL})(null, args)", payload)
    if request.wait_ms:
        await asyncio.sleep(request.wait_ms / 1000)
    return await session.describe("scroll", evidence=evidence)


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\approvals.py
================================================================================

"""Single-use approvals issued only through a trusted, authenticated control path."""

import hashlib
import json
import secrets
import time

from .errors import BrowserError
from .state import capture_state


def request_digest(action, request):
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps([action, request.model_dump()], sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()


class ApprovalStore:
    def __init__(self):
        self._grants = {}

    async def scope(self, session):
        return [session.session_id, session.generation, session.tabs.active,
                (await capture_state(session.tabs.page()))["fingerprint"]]

    async def issue(self, session, action, request):
        now = time.monotonic()
        self._grants = {key: value for key, value in self._grants.items() if value[0] > now}
        if len(self._grants) >= 100:
            raise BrowserError("APPROVAL_LIMIT", "Too many outstanding approvals")
        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        self._grants[token] = (now + 120, await self.scope(session), request_digest(action, request))
        return token

    async def consume(self, token, session, action, request):
        grant = self._grants.pop(token, None)
        if not grant or grant[0] < time.monotonic() or grant[1] != await self.scope(session) or grant[2] != request_digest(action, request):
            raise BrowserError("APPROVAL_INVALID", "Approval is expired, consumed, or does not match the reviewed action/state")


SENSITIVE = """el => {
    const form = el?.form || el?.closest?.('form');
    const roots = [el, form].filter(Boolean);
    const selector = 'input[type=password],input[type=file],[autocomplete^="cc-"],[autocomplete="transaction-amount"],[data-xbrowser-sensitive]';
    return roots.some(root => root.matches?.(selector) || root.querySelector?.(selector)) ||
        /^(pay|purchase|buy|place order|donate|delete account)\b/i.test((el?.innerText || el?.getAttribute?.('aria-label') || '').trim());
}"""


async def guard_target(session, target):
    if await target.evaluate("el => el.matches('input[type=file]')"):
        raise BrowserError("UPLOAD_DISABLED", "File uploads are disabled")
    if await target.evaluate(SENSITIVE) and not session._approved:
        raise BrowserError("HUMAN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED", "This credential, payment, upload or sensitive form action requires a reviewed approval")


async def guard_key(session, page, key):
    modifiers = key.split("+")
    base = modifiers[-1]
    document_keys = {"Enter", "Tab", "Escape", "Space", "Backspace", "Delete", "ArrowUp", "ArrowDown", "ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "Home", "End", "PageUp", "PageDown", "NumpadEnter"}
    safe = (len(modifiers) == 1 and (len(base) == 1 or base in document_keys)) or (
        modifiers[:-1] == ["Shift"] and (len(base) == 1 or base in document_keys)
    ) or (modifiers[:-1] in (["Control"], ["Meta"]) and base.lower() in {"a", "c", "x", "v", "f", "backspace", "delete", "home", "end", "enter"})
    if not safe:
        raise BrowserError("KEY_DENIED", "Browser chrome, developer tools, file dialogs and system shortcuts are disabled")
    for frame in page.frames:
        if not session._approved and key not in {"Escape", "Tab", "Shift+Tab"} and await frame.evaluate(f"() => ({SENSITIVE})(document.activeElement)"):
            raise BrowserError("HUMAN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED", "Keyboard interaction with a sensitive form requires a reviewed approval")


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\controller.py
================================================================================

"""Shared validated action execution. The public boundary owns the lock once."""

import asyncio
import hashlib
import json

from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError
from playwright.async_api import TimeoutError as PlaywrightTimeout

from . import actions
from .errors import BrowserError
from .observe import observe
from .schemas import BrowserResponse, ObserveRequest
from .state import capture_state
from .token_budget import token_count


def failure(session, action, exc, *, code=None):
    if code is None:
        code = exc.code if isinstance(exc, BrowserError) else (
            "ACTION_TIMEOUT" if isinstance(exc, (TimeoutError, PlaywrightTimeout)) else "BROWSER_ERROR"
        )
    uncertain = code == "ACTION_TIMEOUT"
    return BrowserResponse(success=False, action=action, error=str(exc) or code, error_code=code,
                           output="Action timed out; observe before retrying. Side effects may have completed." if uncertain else "",
                           data={"outcome_uncertain": True} if uncertain else {},
                           session_id=session.session_id, generation=session.generation,
                           tab_id=session.tabs.active, restarted=session._restarted)


async def state(session):
    data = {"session": session.session_id, "generation": session.generation, "tab": session.tabs.active}
    if session.tabs.active:
        data["page"] = (await capture_state(session.tabs.page()))["fingerprint"]
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]


async def dispatch(session, action, request):
    """Internal: caller must already own session.operation(). Never retries."""
    session.policy.check(action)
    if not session.running:
        raise BrowserError("SESSION_LOST", "Browser stopped during the sequence; observe after restarting")
    if session.tabs.active is None:
        await session._context.new_page()
    page = session.tabs.page()
    before = await state(session)
    if action == "open":
        result = await session._open(page, **request.model_dump())
    elif action == "tabs":
        result = await session._tab_action(page, **request.model_dump())
    elif action == "observe":
        result = await observe(session, page, request)
    elif action == "screenshot":
        observation = await observe(session, page, ObserveRequest()) if request.marks else None
        path = await session.capture(page, **request.model_dump(), annotations=observation.data["elements"] if observation else None)
        result = await session.describe(action, artifacts=[str(path)], evidence={
            "file_exists": path.is_file(), "size_bytes": path.stat().st_size,
        })
        result.output = f"Screenshot saved to {path}\n" + result.output
        if observation:
            result.snapshot_id = observation.snapshot_id
            result.data["elements"] = observation.data["elements"]
            result.output += "\n" + observation.output
    else:
        method = {"click": actions.click, "type": actions.type_text, "press": actions.press,
                  "wait": actions.wait, "scroll": actions.scroll}[action]
        result = await method(session, page, request)
    # All actions expose the same scope-aware state, including navigation/tabs.
    previous_state = result.state_after
    result.state_before = before
    try:
        result.state_after = await state(session)
        result.state_changed = result.state_after != before
        if action == "observe" and previous_state:
            result.output = result.output.replace(f"State: {previous_state}", f"State: {result.state_after}")
            result.data["token_count"] = token_count(result.output)
    except (BrowserError, PlaywrightError):
        result.data["warning"] = "Action completed; subsequent browser state is unavailable. Observe before proceeding."
    return result


async def execute(session, action, request, *, approval_token=None):
    try:
        session.policy.check(action)  # Deny without launching a browser.
        async with asyncio.timeout(35):
            async with session.operation():
                if approval_token:
                    await session.approvals.consume(approval_token, session, action, request)
                    session._approved = True
                return await dispatch(session, action, request)
    except (BrowserError, PlaywrightError, OSError, ValueError, TimeoutError) as exc:
        return failure(session, action, exc)

"""Machine-readable browser failures, independent of the HTTP transport."""


class BrowserError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\images.py
================================================================================

"""Confined, bounded screenshot transfer for the local vision fallback."""

import base64
import io

from PIL import Image

from .errors import BrowserError


def image_data(session, path):
    target = session.artifact_path(path)
    if target.suffix.lower() != ".png" or not target.is_file() or target.stat().st_size > 20 * 1024 * 1024:
        raise BrowserError("INVALID_IMAGE", "Expected an existing workspace PNG no larger than 20 MiB")
    try:
        with Image.open(target) as picture:
            if picture.format != "PNG" or picture.width * picture.height > 16_000_000:
                raise BrowserError("INVALID_IMAGE", "PNG dimensions exceed the vision transfer limit")
            picture.thumbnail((1280, 1280))
            buffer = io.BytesIO()
            picture.convert("RGB").save(buffer, format="PNG")
            return {"image_base64": base64.b64encode(buffer.getvalue()).decode(), "width": picture.width, "height": picture.height}
    except (OSError, Image.DecompressionBombError) as exc:
        raise BrowserError("INVALID_IMAGE", "Unable to decode screenshot") from exc


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\macros.py
================================================================================

"""Bounded sequential execution, partial evidence, and no automatic replay."""

import asyncio
from contextlib import suppress

from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError

from .controller import dispatch, failure
from .errors import BrowserError
from .schemas import BrowserResponse


async def run_connected(session, request, connection):
    """FastAPI does not automatically cancel handlers when a client disconnects."""
    task = asyncio.create_task(run(session, request))
    try:
        while not task.done():
            await asyncio.wait({task}, timeout=0.2)
            if not task.done() and await connection.is_disconnected():
                raise asyncio.CancelledError("Macro client disconnected; remaining steps cancelled")
        return await task
    finally:
        if not task.done():
            task.cancel()
        with suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
            await task


async def run(session, request):
    results: list[BrowserResponse] = []
    current = None
    failed_index = None
    try:
        # Preflight capabilities for the entire macro before its first side effect.
        for step in request.steps:
            session.policy.check(step.tool)
        async with asyncio.timeout(request.timeout_ms / 1000):
            async with session.operation():
                for index, step in enumerate(request.steps):
                    current = step.tool
                    failed_index = index
                    try:
                        async with asyncio.timeout(30):
                            result = await dispatch(session, step.tool, step.args)
                    except (BrowserError, PlaywrightError, OSError, ValueError, TimeoutError) as exc:
                        result = failure(session, step.tool, exc)
                    results.append(result)
                    current = None
                    if not result.success:
                        break
                else:
                    failed_index = None
    except (BrowserError, PlaywrightError, OSError, ValueError, TimeoutError) as exc:
        if current is not None:
            results.append(failure(session, current, exc))
        else:
            # Failure before dispatch (policy/lock/startup) consumes no sub-actions.
            result = failure(session, "run", exc)
            result.data = {"results": [], "attempted_actions": 0, "failed_index": None}
            return result
    success = failed_index is None
    failed = results[-1] if not success else None
    output = "\n".join(f"{index + 1}. {item.action}: {'ok' if item.success else item.error_code}" for index, item in enumerate(results))
    artifacts = [path for item in results for path in item.artifacts]
    if artifacts:
        output += "\nArtifacts:\n" + "\n".join(artifacts)
    return BrowserResponse(
        success=success, action="run", output=output,
        error=failed.error if failed else None, error_code=failed.error_code if failed else None,
        session_id=session.session_id, generation=session.generation, tab_id=session.tabs.active,
        restarted=any(item.restarted for item in results), artifacts=artifacts,
        state_before=results[0].state_before if results else None,
        state_after=results[-1].state_after if results else None,
        state_changed=bool(results and results[-1].state_after is not None and results[0].state_before != results[-1].state_after),
        data={"results": [item.model_dump() for item in results], "attempted_actions": len(results),
              "failed_index": failed_index, "completed_actions": sum(item.success for item in results),
              "outcome_uncertain": bool(failed and failed.data.get("outcome_uncertain"))},
        action_verification={"sequence_completed": success, "artifacts_created": len(artifacts)},
    )

FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\marks.py
