"""Read-only DOM discovery and bounded, snapshot-scoped handle ownership."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import OrderedDict
from contextlib import suppress
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError

from .errors import BrowserError

# Only fixed, application-owned JavaScript runs here. Nothing supplied by the model
# is evaluated. No DOM attributes, event listeners, or global variables are installed.
INSPECT = r"""el => {
    const clean = value => (value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const root = el.getRootNode();
    const labelled = (el.getAttribute('aria-labelledby') || '').split(/\s+/)
        .map(id => root.getElementById?.(id)?.textContent || '').join(' ');
    const tag = el.localName;
    const type = el.getAttribute('type') || '';
    const implicit = {a:el.hasAttribute('href')?'link':'', button:'button', textarea:'textbox',
        select:el.multiple?'listbox':'combobox', summary:'button',
        input:({checkbox:'checkbox',radio:'radio',range:'slider',number:'spinbutton',
            button:'button',submit:'button',reset:'button',search:'searchbox'})[type] || 'textbox'};
    const role = el.getAttribute('role') || implicit[tag] || (el.isContentEditable?'textbox':'generic');
    const labels = Array.from(el.labels || []).map(label => label.textContent).join(' ');
    const name = clean(clean(labelled) || el.getAttribute('aria-label') || clean(labels) || el.getAttribute('alt') ||
        (['button','submit','reset'].includes(type)?el.value:'') ||
        (['input','textarea','select'].includes(tag)?'':el.textContent) ||
        el.getAttribute('title') || el.getAttribute('placeholder') || el.querySelector('img[alt]')?.getAttribute('alt'));
    const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const style = getComputedStyle(el);
    const visible = el.isConnected && rect.width > 0 && rect.height > 0 &&
        el.checkVisibility({checkOpacity:true,checkVisibilityCSS:true}) &&
        style.visibility !== 'hidden' && style.visibility !== 'collapse' && style.display !== 'none' &&
        style.opacity !== '0' && type !== 'hidden' && !el.closest('[inert]');
    const in_viewport = rect.bottom > 0 && rect.right > 0 && rect.top < innerHeight && rect.left < innerWidth;
    // Test a point within the visible portion, then pierce open shadow roots.
    const x = (Math.max(0,rect.left)+Math.min(innerWidth,rect.right))/2;
    const y = (Math.max(0,rect.top)+Math.min(innerHeight,rect.bottom))/2;
    let hit = in_viewport ? document.elementFromPoint(x,y) : null;
    while (hit?.shadowRoot) {
        const deeper = hit.shadowRoot.elementFromPoint(x,y);
        if (!deeper || deeper === hit) break;
        hit = deeper;
    }
    let covered = in_viewport;
    for (let current=hit; current; current=current.parentNode || current.host) {
        if (current === el) { covered=false; break; }
    }
    const disabled = !!el.disabled || el.matches(':disabled') || el.getAttribute('aria-disabled') === 'true';
    return {role, name:name.slice(0,80), tag, type, visible, in_viewport, covered, disabled,
        checked:['checkbox','radio'].includes(type)?el.checked:
            (el.hasAttribute('aria-checked')?el.getAttribute('aria-checked')==='true':null),
        value:(type === 'password' || /^(cc-|transaction-)/i.test(el.autocomplete || '') ||
            /password|secret|token|card|cvv|cvc/i.test(el.name || el.id || '')) ? null :
            (['input','textarea','select'].includes(tag)?el.value.slice(0,40):null),
        bbox:{x:rect.x,y:rect.y,width:rect.width,height:rect.height},
        identity:JSON.stringify([tag,role,name.slice(0,500),type,el.id,el.getAttribute('href'),
            el.getAttribute('formaction'),el.getAttribute('onclick'),el.getAttribute('contenteditable')])};
}"""

COLLECT = """({scope, limit}) => {
    const inspect = INSPECT;
    const selector = 'a[href],button,input,textarea,select,summary,[role],[onclick],[tabindex],[contenteditable]';
    const roles = new Set(['button','link','tab','menuitem','menuitemcheckbox','menuitemradio',
        'checkbox','radio','textbox','searchbox','combobox','listbox','option','switch','slider','spinbutton','treeitem']);
    const elements=[], info=[];
    let above=0, below=0, covered=0, visited=0, truncated=false;
    const walk = root => {
        for (const el of root.querySelectorAll('*')) {
            if (++visited > 12000) { truncated=true; return; }
            if (el.matches(selector) && (el.matches('a[href],button,input,textarea,select,summary,[onclick]') ||
                roles.has(el.getAttribute('role')) || el.tabIndex >= 0 || el.isContentEditable)) {
                const data=inspect(el);
                if (data.visible) {
                    if (data.bbox.y + data.bbox.height <= 0) above++;
                    else if (data.bbox.y >= innerHeight) below++;
                    if (data.in_viewport && data.covered) covered++;
                    if ((scope === 'page' || data.in_viewport) && !data.covered) {
                        if (elements.length < limit) { elements.push(el); info.push(data); }
                        else truncated=true;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (el.shadowRoot) walk(el.shadowRoot);
        }
    };
    walk(document);
    return {elements, meta:{info, above, below, covered, truncated}};
}""".replace("INSPECT", INSPECT)


@dataclass
class Reference:
    handle: Any
    info: dict
    frame_id: str


@dataclass
class Snapshot:
    snapshot_id: str
    session_id: str
    generation: int
    tab_id: str
    refs: dict[int, Reference] = field(default_factory=dict)
    valid: bool = True

    async def dispose(self):
        self.valid = False
        for reference in self.refs.values():
            with suppress(PlaywrightError):
                await reference.handle.dispose()
        self.refs.clear()


class ReferenceRegistry:
    def __init__(self):
        self._snapshots: OrderedDict[str, Snapshot] = OrderedDict()
        self._counter = 0

    def invalidate(self, tab_id: str):
        if tab_id in self._snapshots:
            self._snapshots[tab_id].valid = False

    async def clear(self):
        for snapshot in self._snapshots.values():
            await snapshot.dispose()
        self._snapshots.clear()

    async def create(self, session_id: str, generation: int, tab_id: str) -> Snapshot:
        for old_tab, snapshot in list(self._snapshots.items()):
            if not snapshot.valid or old_tab == tab_id:
                await snapshot.dispose()
                del self._snapshots[old_tab]
        while len(self._snapshots) >= 8:
            _, oldest = self._snapshots.popitem(last=False)
            await oldest.dispose()
        self._counter += 1
        snapshot = Snapshot(f"s{self._counter}-{session_id[:12]}", session_id, generation, tab_id)
        self._snapshots[tab_id] = snapshot
        return snapshot

    async def resolve(self, session, snapshot_id: str, ref: int):
        snapshot = self._snapshots.get(session.tabs.active)
        if (snapshot is None or not snapshot.valid or snapshot.snapshot_id != snapshot_id or
                snapshot.session_id != session.session_id or snapshot.generation != session.generation):
            raise BrowserError("STALE_REF", "Snapshot does not match this active tab/session; call browser_observe")
        reference = snapshot.refs.get(ref)
        if reference is None:
            raise BrowserError("INVALID_REF", "Reference was not reported in this snapshot; call browser_observe")
        try:
            current = await reference.handle.evaluate(INSPECT)
        except PlaywrightError as exc:
            raise BrowserError("STALE_REF", "Target document changed; call browser_observe") from exc
        if not current["visible"] or current["identity"] != reference.info["identity"]:
            raise BrowserError("STALE_REF", "Target was removed, hidden, or changed identity; call browser_observe")
        if current["disabled"] or current["covered"]:
            raise BrowserError("TARGET_NOT_ACTIONABLE", "Target is disabled or covered; call browser_observe")
        frame = await reference.handle.owner_frame()
        try:
            while frame and frame.parent_frame:
                host = await frame.frame_element()
                try:
                    geometry = await host.evaluate(INSPECT)
                    if not geometry["visible"] or geometry["covered"]:
                        raise BrowserError("TARGET_NOT_ACTIONABLE", "Target frame is hidden or covered; call browser_observe")
                finally:
                    await host.dispose()
                frame = frame.parent_frame
        except PlaywrightError as exc:
            raise BrowserError("STALE_REF", "Target frame changed; call browser_observe") from exc
        return reference.handle


async def discover(page, *, scope: str, limit: int = 120):
    """Walk supported frames, retaining handles but never mutating the document."""
    found: list[Reference] = []
    try:
        return await _discover(page, scope=scope, limit=limit, found=found)
    except BaseException:
        for reference in found:
            with suppress(PlaywrightError):
                await reference.handle.dispose()
        raise


async def _discover(page, *, scope, limit, found):
    counts = {"above": 0, "below": 0, "covered": 0}
    warnings = []
    viewport = await page.evaluate("() => ({width:innerWidth,height:innerHeight})")
    for index, frame in enumerate(page.frames[:16], 1):
        if len(found) >= limit:
            warnings.append("Element limit reached")
            break
        batch = array = None
        try:
            offset = {"x": 0, "y": 0}
            if frame.parent_frame:
                ancestor = frame
                while ancestor.parent_frame:
                    host = await ancestor.frame_element()
                    try:
                        geometry = await host.evaluate(INSPECT)
                        if not geometry["visible"] or geometry["covered"] or (
                                scope == "viewport" and not geometry["in_viewport"]):
                            break
                    finally:
                        await host.dispose()
                    ancestor = ancestor.parent_frame
                else:
                    ancestor = None
                if ancestor is not None:
                    continue
                host = await frame.frame_element()
                try:
                    offset = await host.bounding_box() or offset
                finally:
                    await host.dispose()
            batch = await frame.evaluate_handle(COLLECT, {"scope": scope, "limit": limit - len(found)})
            meta = await batch.evaluate("batch => batch.meta")
            for key in counts:
                counts[key] += meta[key]
            if meta["truncated"]:
                warnings.append(f"f{index}: discovery limit reached")
            array = await batch.get_property("elements")
            properties = await array.get_properties()
            for key, handle in properties.items():
                info = meta["info"][int(key)]
                info["bbox"]["x"] += offset["x"]
                info["bbox"]["y"] += offset["y"]
                box = info["bbox"]
                info["in_viewport"] = info["in_viewport"] and (
                    box["y"] < viewport["height"] and box["y"] + box["height"] > 0 and
                    box["x"] < viewport["width"] and box["x"] + box["width"] > 0
                )
                found.append(Reference(handle.as_element(), info, f"f{index}"))
        except PlaywrightError:
            warnings.append(f"f{index}: frame unavailable")
        finally:
            if array:
                await array.dispose()
            if batch:
                await batch.dispose()
    if len(page.frames) > 16:
        warnings.append("Frame limit reached")
    found.sort(key=lambda item: (not item.info["in_viewport"], round(item.info["bbox"]["y"] / 8),
                                 item.info["bbox"]["x"]))
    return found, counts, warnings


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\observe.py
================================================================================

"""Compact observations with a hard character budget and exact reported refs."""

import json
from contextlib import suppress

from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError

from .errors import BrowserError
from .marks import discover
from .state import capture_state
from .token_budget import token_count


def quoted(value: str, limit: int) -> str:
    value = value[:limit]
    rendered = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)
    while len(rendered) > limit:
        value = value[:len(value) // 2]
        rendered = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)
    return rendered


async def observe(session, page, request):
    snapshot = await session.references.create(session.session_id, session.generation, session.tabs.active)
    references = []
    completed = False
    try:
        references, counts, warnings = await discover(page, scope=request.scope)
        state = await capture_state(page)
        lines = [
            "Page data (untrusted)",
            f"Tab: {snapshot.tab_id} | Generation: {snapshot.generation}",
            f"Snapshot: {snapshot.snapshot_id} | State: {state['fingerprint']}",
            f"URL: {quoted(state['url'], 220)}",
            f"Title: {quoted(state['title'], 100)}",
            f"Scroll: {state['y']}/{state['scroll_height']} | top={state['at_top']} bottom={state['at_bottom']}",
            "Elements:",
        ]
        elements = []
        # Reserve space for counts and truncation hints. Retain only refs actually
        # returned to the caller, so hidden/omitted indices cannot be guessed.
        for reference in references:
            info = reference.info
            ref = len(elements) + 1
            flags = " disabled" if info["disabled"] else ""
            if info["checked"] is not None:
                flags += f" checked={info['checked']}"
            if info["value"] is not None:
                flags += f" value={quoted(info['value'], 20)}"
            line = f"[{ref}] {reference.frame_id} {info['role'][:24]} {quoted(info['name'], 40)}{flags}"
            if len(elements) >= request.max_elements or sum(len(item) + 1 for item in lines) + len(line) > request.max_chars - 240:
                break
            lines.append(line)
            snapshot.refs[ref] = reference
            elements.append({"ref": ref, "frame_id": reference.frame_id,
                             **{key: info[key] for key in ("role", "name", "type", "disabled", "checked", "value", "bbox")}})
        if request.text:
            text = await page.locator("body").inner_text(timeout=5000)
            available = max(0, request.max_chars - sum(len(item) + 1 for item in lines) - 240)
            if available > 30:
                lines.append("Text: " + quoted(text, max(0, available // 2 - 10)))
        def footer():
            omitted = len(references) - len(elements)
            return f"More: {counts['above']} above, {counts['below']} below, {omitted} omitted; covered={counts['covered']}"

        if warnings:
            lines.append("Hints: " + "; ".join(warnings)[:120])
        budget = 700
        # Reserve 20 tokens for the controller's final scope-aware state hash.
        while (token_count("\n".join([*lines, footer()])) or 0) > budget - 20:
            text_index = next((i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.startswith("Text: ")), None)
            if text_index is not None:
                lines.pop(text_index)
            elif elements:
                element = elements.pop()
                del snapshot.refs[element["ref"]]
                lines = [line for line in lines if not line.startswith(f"[{element['ref']}]")]
            else:
                raise BrowserError("OBSERVATION_BUDGET", "Page header exceeds the configured token budget")
        omitted = len(references) - len(elements)
        lines.append(footer())
        if not snapshot.valid:
            raise BrowserError("STALE_SNAPSHOT", "Page navigated while observing; call browser_observe again")
        result = await session.describe("observe")
        result.output = "\n".join(lines)[:request.max_chars]
        result.snapshot_id = snapshot.snapshot_id
        result.state_after = state["fingerprint"]
        result.data.update({"elements": elements, "scroll": {key: state[key] for key in (
            "x", "y", "height", "scroll_height", "at_top", "at_bottom",
        )}, "counts": {**counts, "omitted": omitted}, "warnings": warnings,
            "output_chars": len(result.output), "token_estimate": (len(result.output) + 3) // 4,
            "token_count": token_count(result.output), "token_limit": budget,
            "tokenizer": "Qwen3.5" if token_count(result.output) is not None else "character_estimate"})
        completed = True
        return result
    finally:
        if not completed:
            snapshot.valid = False
        kept = {id(reference) for reference in snapshot.refs.values()}
        for reference in references:
            if id(reference) not in kept:
                with suppress(PlaywrightError):
                    await reference.handle.dispose()


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\proxy.py
================================================================================

"""Browser-only egress proxy: validate DNS results and connect to a pinned IP.

TLS stays end-to-end between Chromium and the destination. No certificate
interception, credential logging, or ambient environment proxy configuration.
"""

import asyncio
from contextlib import suppress
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from .errors import BrowserError


class EgressProxy:
    def __init__(self, policy, events):
        self.policy = policy
        self.events = events
        self._server = None
        self._tasks = set()

    async def start(self):
        self._server = await asyncio.start_server(self._accept, "127.0.0.1", 0, limit=65536)
        return f"http://127.0.0.1:{self._server.sockets[0].getsockname()[1]}"

    async def stop(self):
        if self._server:
            self._server.close()
            await self._server.wait_closed()
            self._server = None
        tasks = list(self._tasks)
        for task in tasks:
            task.cancel()
        if tasks:
            await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)

    async def _accept(self, reader, writer):
        if len(self._tasks) >= 128:
            writer.close()
            await writer.wait_closed()
            return
        task = asyncio.current_task()
        self._tasks.add(task)
        upstream = None
        connected = False
        try:
            async with asyncio.timeout(15):
                header = await reader.readuntil(b"\r\n\r\n")
                lines = header.decode("latin1").split("\r\n")
                method, target, version = lines[0].split(" ", 2)
                if version not in {"HTTP/1.0", "HTTP/1.1"}:
                    raise BrowserError("NAVIGATION_DENIED", "Unsupported proxy protocol")
                url = f"https://{target}/" if method == "CONNECT" else target
                ips, port = await self.policy.resolve_addresses(url)
                for ip in ips:
                    try:
                        source, upstream = await asyncio.open_connection(ip, port)
                        break
                    except OSError:
                        if ip == ips[-1]:
                            raise
                if method == "CONNECT":
                    writer.write(b"HTTP/1.1 200 Connection Established\r\n\r\n")
                    await writer.drain()
                else:
                    parsed = urlsplit(url)
                    path = parsed.path or "/"
                    if parsed.query:
                        path += "?" + parsed.query
                    clean = []
                    for line in lines[1:]:
                        if not line:
                            continue
                        name = line.split(":", 1)[0].lower()
                        if name not in {"proxy-connection", "proxy-authorization", "connection", "host"}:
                            clean.append(line)
                    clean += [f"Host: {parsed.netloc}", "Connection: close"]
                    upstream.write((f"{method} {path} {version}\r\n" + "\r\n".join(clean) + "\r\n\r\n").encode("latin1"))
                    await upstream.drain()
                connected = True
            # No 15s lifetime cap on active TLS/page transfers; operation deadlines
            # live in the controller. Cleanup closes all tunnels on session stop.
            async def copy(source_reader, destination):
                while data := await source_reader.read(65536):
                    destination.write(data)
                    await destination.drain()

            first = asyncio.create_task(copy(reader, upstream))
            second = asyncio.create_task(copy(source, writer))
            try:
                await asyncio.wait({first, second}, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)
            finally:
                for transfer in (first, second):
                    transfer.cancel()
                await asyncio.gather(first, second, return_exceptions=True)
        except (BrowserError, OSError, ValueError, TimeoutError, asyncio.IncompleteReadError, asyncio.LimitOverrunError) as exc:
            if isinstance(exc, BrowserError):
                self.events.append({"type": "navigation_denied", "code": exc.code})
            if not connected:
                with suppress(OSError):
                    writer.write(b"HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: 0\r\n\r\n")
                    await writer.drain()
        finally:
            for stream in (upstream, writer):
                if stream:
                    stream.close()
                    with suppress(OSError):
                        await stream.wait_closed()
            self._tasks.discard(task)


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\safety.py
================================================================================

"""Sandbox-owned capabilities. Request bodies cannot grant additional authority."""

import asyncio
import ipaddress
import os
import socket
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from .errors import BrowserError

CORE_ACTIONS = frozenset({"open", "observe", "click", "type", "press", "scroll", "tabs", "wait", "screenshot"})


def public_address(value):
    address = ipaddress.ip_address(value)
    if not address.is_global or address.is_multicast or address.is_reserved:
        return False
    if isinstance(address, ipaddress.IPv6Address):
        embedded = address.ipv4_mapped or address.sixtofour
        if address in ipaddress.ip_network("64:ff9b::/96"):
            embedded = ipaddress.IPv4Address(int(address) & 0xffffffff)
        if embedded and not public_address(str(embedded)):
            return False
        if address.teredo and not all(public_address(str(item)) for item in address.teredo):
            return False
    return True


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BrowserPolicy:
    allowed_actions: frozenset[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: CORE_ACTIONS)
    allowed_domains: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    denied_domains: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    local_origins: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Trusted, exact fixture/service exceptions; never supplied by the model.

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls):
        def values(name):
            return tuple(item.strip() for item in os.environ.get(name, "").split(",") if item.strip())
        origins = []
        for origin in values("XBROWSER_LOCAL_ORIGINS"):
            parsed = urlsplit(origin)
            if parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not parsed.hostname or parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.path not in {"", "/"} or parsed.query or parsed.fragment:
                raise ValueError("XBROWSER_LOCAL_ORIGINS must contain exact HTTP(S) origins")
            host = parsed.hostname.lower()
            origins.append(f"{parsed.scheme}://{'['+host+']' if ':' in host else host}:{parsed.port or (443 if parsed.scheme == 'https' else 80)}")
        return cls(
            allowed_actions=frozenset(values("XBROWSER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS")) if "XBROWSER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS" in os.environ else CORE_ACTIONS,
            allowed_domains=values("XBROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS"), denied_domains=values("XBROWSER_DENIED_DOMAINS"),
            local_origins=tuple(origins),
        )

    def check(self, action: str):
        if action not in self.allowed_actions:
            raise BrowserError("ACTION_DENIED", f"Browser action disabled by sandbox policy: {action}")

    def destination(self, url: str) -> tuple[str, int, bool]:
        parsed = urlsplit(url)
        if parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https", "ws", "wss"} or not parsed.hostname or parsed.username or parsed.password:
            raise BrowserError("NAVIGATION_DENIED", "Only HTTP(S) destinations without embedded credentials are allowed")
        host = parsed.hostname.rstrip(".").encode("idna").decode().lower()
        if "%" in host:
            raise BrowserError("NAVIGATION_DENIED", "Scoped network addresses are forbidden")
        port = parsed.port or (443 if parsed.scheme in {"https", "wss"} else 80)
        scheme = {"ws": "http", "wss": "https"}.get(parsed.scheme, parsed.scheme)
        origin = f"{scheme}://{'[' + host + ']' if ':' in host else host}:{port}"
        local = origin in self.local_origins

        def matches(pattern):
            pattern = pattern.lower().rstrip(".")
            return host == pattern or (pattern.startswith("*.") and host.endswith(pattern[1:]) and host != pattern[2:])

        if any(matches(pattern) for pattern in self.denied_domains):
            raise BrowserError("NAVIGATION_DENIED", "Destination is blocked by domain policy")
        if self.allowed_domains and not any(matches(pattern) for pattern in self.allowed_domains) and not local:
            raise BrowserError("NAVIGATION_DENIED", "Destination is outside the domain allowlist")
        if not local and port not in {80, 443}:
            raise BrowserError("NAVIGATION_DENIED", "Destination port is not permitted")
        return host, port, local

    async def resolve(self, url: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
        ips, port = await self.resolve_addresses(url)
        return ips[0], port

    async def resolve_addresses(self, url: str) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
        host, port, local = self.destination(url)
        try:
            addresses = await asyncio.wait_for(asyncio.get_running_loop().getaddrinfo(
                host, port, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM,
            ), 5)
        except (OSError, TimeoutError) as exc:
            raise BrowserError("NAVIGATION_DENIED", "Destination could not be resolved") from exc
        ips = list(dict.fromkeys(item[4][0] for item in addresses))
        if not ips or (not local and any(not public_address(address) for address in ips)):
            raise BrowserError("NAVIGATION_DENIED", "Private, loopback, reserved and metadata addresses are forbidden")
        # The proxy connects to this literal IP, never re-resolving the hostname.
        return ips, port


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\schemas.py
================================================================================

"""Validated foundation contracts; legacy output/error fields remain compatible."""

from typing import Annotated, Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator


class Request(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class SessionRequest(Request):
    action: Literal["start", "stop", "restart", "health"] = "start"


class OpenRequest(Request):
    url: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=8192)
    new_tab: bool = False
    wait_for: Literal["load", "domcontentloaded", "networkidle", "commit"] = "load"


class TabsRequest(Request):
    action: Literal["list", "new", "switch", "close", "back", "forward", "reload"] = "list"
    tab: str | None = None
    url: str | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def target_required(self):
        if self.action in {"switch", "close"} and not self.tab:
            raise ValueError("tab is required for switch and close")
        if self.url is not None and self.action != "new":
            raise ValueError("url is only supported for new tabs")
        return self


class ScreenshotRequest(Request):
    full_page: bool = False
    selector: str | None = None
    path: str | None = None
    marks: bool = False

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def mark_scope(self):
        if self.marks and self.selector:
            raise ValueError("Marked screenshots capture the page, not an element selector")
        return self


class ObserveRequest(Request):
    scope: Literal["viewport", "page"] = "viewport"
    text: bool = False
    max_elements: int = Field(default=30, ge=1, le=100)
    max_chars: int = Field(default=2400, ge=800, le=8000)


class TargetRequest(Request):
    selector: str | None = None
    ref: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    snapshot_id: str | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def reference_pair(self):
        if (self.ref is None) != (self.snapshot_id is None):
            raise ValueError("ref and snapshot_id must be provided together")
        if self.ref is not None and self.selector is not None:
            raise ValueError("Choose a reference or a selector")
        return self


class ClickRequest(TargetRequest):
    text: str | None = None
    role: str | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def click_target(self):
        if self.ref is not None and (self.text is not None or self.role is not None):
            raise ValueError("Reference targeting cannot be mixed with text or role")
        if not (self.selector or self.text or self.ref):
            raise ValueError("Provide ref + snapshot_id, selector, or text")
        return self


class TypeRequest(TargetRequest):
    text: str
    clear_first: bool = True
    press_enter: bool = False

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def type_target(self):
        if not (self.selector or self.ref):
            raise ValueError("Provide ref + snapshot_id or selector")
        return self


class PressRequest(Request):
    key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)


class WaitRequest(Request):
    condition: Literal["text", "selector", "idle", "ms"]
    value: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=2000)
    timeout_ms: int = Field(default=5000, ge=1, le=30000)
    milliseconds: int = Field(default=400, ge=0, le=10000)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def wait_target(self):
        if self.condition in {"text", "selector"} and not self.value:
            raise ValueError("text/selector waits require value")
        return self


class ScrollRequest(TargetRequest):
    direction: Literal["up", "down", "left", "right", "top", "bottom"] = "down"
    pixels: int = Field(default=600, ge=0, le=10000)
    wait_ms: int = Field(default=100, ge=0, le=5000)


class OpenStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["open"]
    args: OpenRequest


class ObserveStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["observe"]
    args: ObserveRequest = Field(default_factory=ObserveRequest)


class ClickStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["click"]
    args: ClickRequest


class TypeStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["type"]
    args: TypeRequest


class PressStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["press"]
    args: PressRequest


class ScrollStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["scroll"]
    args: ScrollRequest = Field(default_factory=ScrollRequest)


class TabsStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["tabs"]
    args: TabsRequest = Field(default_factory=TabsRequest)


class WaitStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["wait"]
    args: WaitRequest


class ScreenshotStep(Request):
    tool: Literal["screenshot"]
    args: ScreenshotRequest = Field(default_factory=ScreenshotRequest)


BrowserStep = Annotated[
    OpenStep | ObserveStep | ClickStep | TypeStep | PressStep | ScrollStep | TabsStep | WaitStep | ScreenshotStep,
    Field(discriminator="tool"),
]


class ApprovalRequest(Request):
    tool: Literal["click", "type", "press"]
    args: dict[str, Any]


class ImageRequest(Request):
    path: str


class ArtifactCheck(Request):
    kind: Literal["artifact"]
    path: str = Field(min_length=1)
    min_bytes: int = Field(default=1, ge=1)
    contains: str | None = None
    sha256: str | None = Field(default=None, pattern=r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    png: bool = False
    width: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
    height: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)


class PageCheck(Request):
    kind: Literal["url", "text", "tab_count"]
    expected: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=10000)


class VerificationRequest(Request):
    checks: list[Annotated[ArtifactCheck | PageCheck, Field(discriminator="kind")]] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=50)


class RunRequest(Request):
    steps: list[BrowserStep] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=20)
    timeout_ms: int = Field(default=60000, ge=1, le=90000)
    action_budget: int = Field(default=20, ge=1, le=20)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def within_allowance(self):
        if len(self.steps) > self.action_budget:
            raise ValueError("Macro exceeds its action allowance")
        return self


class TabInfo(BaseModel):
    tab_id: str
    url: str
    title: str
    active: bool


class BrowserResponse(BaseModel):
    success: bool
    output: str = ""
    error: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    action: str = ""
    session_id: str = ""
    generation: int = 0
    tab_id: str | None = None
    restarted: bool = False
    snapshot_id: str | None = None
    state_before: str | None = None
    state_after: str | None = None
    state_changed: bool = False
    artifacts: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    data: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    # Mechanical evidence is deliberately distinct from the user's task outcome.
    action_verification: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    task_verification: Literal["not_evaluated", "passed", "failed"] = "not_evaluated"


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\session.py
================================================================================

"""One explicitly scoped persistent context, serialized at the operation boundary.

The sandbox owns this object. Never share its profile with another process.
No mutating browser action is automatically replayed after a failure.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import os
import re
import uuid
from collections import deque
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError

from .approvals import ApprovalStore
from .errors import BrowserError
from .marks import ReferenceRegistry
from .proxy import EgressProxy
from .safety import BrowserPolicy
from .schemas import BrowserResponse
from .tabs import TabRegistry


class BrowserSession:
    def __init__(self, workspace: Path | str = "/workspace", *, scope: str = "default", headless: bool = False):
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}", scope):
            raise ValueError("scope must contain 1–64 letters, digits, underscores or hyphens")
        self.workspace = Path(workspace).resolve()
        self.profile = self.workspace / ".xbrowser" / scope / "profile"
        self.headless = headless
        self.session_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        self.generation = 0
        self.tabs = TabRegistry()
        self.references = ReferenceRegistry()
        self.policy = BrowserPolicy()
        self.approvals = ApprovalStore()
        self._approved = False
        self._proxy = None
        self._navigation_error = None
        self.max_tabs = 12
        self.events = deque(maxlen=20)
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
        self._playwright = None
        self._context = None
        self._dead = False
        self._restarted = False
        self._profile_lock = None

    def _claim_profile(self):
        if os.name != "posix":
            return
        import fcntl
        owner = (self.profile.parent / "controller.lock").open("a")
        try:
            fcntl.flock(owner, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError as exc:
            owner.close()
            raise BrowserError("PROFILE_IN_USE", "Another controller owns this browser scope") from exc
        self._profile_lock = owner
        wanted = os.fsencode(f"--user-data-dir={self.profile}")
        for process in Path("/proc").iterdir():
            if not process.name.isdigit():
                continue
            try:
                if wanted in (process / "cmdline").read_bytes().split(b"\0"):
                    raise BrowserError("PROFILE_IN_USE", "A running Chromium process owns this profile")
            except (OSError, PermissionError):
                continue
        # Only Chromium's ephemeral singleton symlinks are recovered. Cookies,
        # history and all other persistent profile data remain intact.
        for name in ("SingletonLock", "SingletonSocket", "SingletonCookie"):
            link = self.profile / name
            if link.is_symlink():
                link.unlink()

    @property
    def running(self):
        return self._context is not None and not self._dead

    async def _start(self):
        if self.running:
            return
        recovering = self._context is not None
        await self._stop()
        from playwright.async_api import async_playwright

        if not self.profile.resolve().is_relative_to(self.workspace):
            raise BrowserError("INVALID_PROFILE", "Browser profile must remain inside the workspace")
        self.profile.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
        try:
            self._claim_profile()
            self._playwright = await async_playwright().start()
            self._proxy = EgressProxy(self.policy, self.events)
            proxy_url = await self._proxy.start()
            self._context = await self._playwright.chromium.launch_persistent_context(
                str(self.profile), headless=self.headless,
                viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 720},
                accept_downloads=False,
                service_workers="block",
                proxy={"server": proxy_url, "bypass": "<-loopback>"},
                args=["--disable-dev-shm-usage", "--disable-quic", "--force-webrtc-ip-handling-policy=disable_non_proxied_udp"],
            )
            await self._context.route("**/*", self._guard_request)
            await self._context.add_init_script("""for (const name of ['RTCPeerConnection','webkitRTCPeerConnection']) {
                Object.defineProperty(globalThis,name,{value:undefined,writable:false,configurable:false});
            }""")
            await self._context.route_web_socket("**/*", lambda socket: socket.close(code=1008, reason="WebSockets disabled by browser policy"))
            self.generation += 1
            self._dead = False
            self._restarted = recovering
            self._context.on("close", self._on_closed)
            self._context.on("page", self._register_page)
            for page in self._context.pages:
                self._register_page(page)
            if not self._context.pages:
                await self._context.new_page()
        except BaseException:
            await self._stop()
            raise

    async def _guard_request(self, route):
        request = route.request
        try:
            await self.policy.resolve(request.url)
            if request.method not in {"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"} and not self._approved:
                raise BrowserError("HUMAN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED", "Network writes require a reviewed action approval")
            await route.continue_()
        except BrowserError as exc:
            self.events.append({"type": "request_denied", "code": exc.code})
            if request.is_navigation_request():
                self._navigation_error = exc
            await route.abort("blockedbyclient")

    def _on_closed(self, *_):
        self._dead = True
        self.tabs.clear()

    def _register_page(self, page):
        tab_id = self.tabs.register(page)
        if self.tabs.count > self.max_tabs:
            self.events.append({"type": "tab_limit", "limit": self.max_tabs})
            asyncio.create_task(page.close())
        page.on("framenavigated", lambda *_: self.references.invalidate(tab_id))
        page.on("close", lambda *_: self.references.invalidate(tab_id))
        # Dismiss prompts and confirmations: never approve a site's proposed action.
        page.on("dialog", self._dialog)
        # Interception suppresses native file dialogs; no tool is allowed to set files.
        page.on("filechooser", lambda *_: self.events.append({"type": "upload_blocked"}))
        page.on("pageerror", lambda error: self.events.append({"type": "pageerror", "message": str(error)[:500]}))
        page.on("console", self._console)
        page.on("requestfailed", lambda request: self.events.append({
            "type": "requestfailed", "url": request.url[:500], "message": request.failure,
        }))
        page.on("crash", self._on_crash)

    def _console(self, message):
        if message.type == "error":
            self.events.append({"type": "console", "message": message.text[:500]})

    def _on_crash(self, *_):
        self.events.append({"type": "crash", "generation": self.generation})
        self._dead = True  # Restart before the next action; never replay the interrupted action.

    async def _dialog(self, dialog):
        self.events.append({"type": "dialog", "kind": dialog.type, "message": dialog.message[:500]})
        await dialog.dismiss()

    async def _stop(self):
        await self.references.clear()
        context, playwright = self._context, self._playwright
        self._context = self._playwright = None
        try:
            if context is not None:
                await context.close()
        finally:
            try:
                if playwright is not None:
                    await playwright.stop()
            finally:
                self.tabs.clear()
                self._dead = False
                try:
                    if self._proxy:
                        await self._proxy.stop()
                        self._proxy = None
                finally:
                    if self._profile_lock:
                        self._profile_lock.close()
                        self._profile_lock = None

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def operation(self):
        """Legacy and foundation actions share this exact lock and active page."""
        async with self._lock:
            self._restarted = False
            self._navigation_error = None
            self._approved = False
            await self._start()
            if self.tabs.active is None:
                await self._context.new_page()
            try:
                yield self.tabs.page()
            finally:
                self._approved = False

    @staticmethod
    def validate_url(url: str):
        parsed = urlsplit(url)
        if url == "about:blank":
            return
        if parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not parsed.hostname or parsed.username or parsed.password:
            raise BrowserError("INVALID_URL", "Only HTTP(S) URLs without embedded credentials and about:blank are allowed")

    def artifact_path(self, path: str) -> Path:
        target = Path(path)
        if not target.is_absolute():
            target = self.workspace / target
        target = target.resolve()
        if (not target.is_relative_to(self.workspace) or target.is_relative_to((self.workspace / ".xbrowser").resolve()) or
                target.is_relative_to(self.profile.resolve())):
            raise BrowserError("INVALID_PATH", "Artifacts must stay inside the workspace and outside browser profiles")
        return target

    def screenshot_target(self, path: str | None) -> Path:
        target = self.artifact_path(path or str(Path("screenshots") / f"browser-{uuid.uuid4().hex}.png"))
        if target.suffix.lower() != ".png":
            raise BrowserError("INVALID_PATH", "Screenshots must use a .png extension")
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return target

    async def capture(self, page, *, path=None, full_page=False, selector=None, marks=False, annotations=None) -> Path:
        target = self.screenshot_target(path)
        # Exclusive creation prevents accidental overwrite, including duplicate requests.
        try:
            with target.open("xb") as stream:
                try:
                    if selector:
                        content = await page.locator(selector).screenshot(timeout=5000, type="png")
                    else:
                        content = await page.screenshot(full_page=full_page, type="png", timeout=10000)
                    if marks:
                        import io

                        from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
                        picture = Image.open(io.BytesIO(content)).convert("RGB")
                        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(picture)
                        offset = await page.evaluate("() => [scrollX,scrollY]") if full_page else [0, 0]
                        for item in annotations or []:
                            box = item["bbox"]
                            x, y = box["x"] + offset[0], box["y"] + offset[1]
                            draw.rectangle((x, y, x + box["width"], y + box["height"]), outline="#ffb000", width=2)
                            label = str(item["ref"])
                            draw.rectangle((x, y, x + 8 * len(label) + 5, y + 15), fill="#ffb000")
                            draw.text((x + 2, y + 1), label, fill="black")
                        picture.save(stream, format="PNG")
                    else:
                        stream.write(content)
                except BaseException:
                    stream.close()
                    target.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                    raise
        except FileExistsError as exc:
            raise BrowserError("ARTIFACT_EXISTS", f"Screenshot already exists: {target}") from exc
        return target

    async def describe(self, action: str, *, artifacts=None, evidence=None) -> BrowserResponse:
        tabs = await self.tabs.snapshot()
        data = {"running": self.running, "tabs": [tab.model_dump() for tab in tabs], "events": list(self.events)}
        output = "\n".join(f"{tab.tab_id}{'*' if tab.active else ''}: {tab.title} | {tab.url}" for tab in tabs)
        return BrowserResponse(
            success=True, action=action, output=output, session_id=self.session_id,
            generation=self.generation, tab_id=self.tabs.active, restarted=self._restarted,
            data=data, artifacts=artifacts or [], action_verification=evidence or {},
        )

    async def control(self, action: str) -> BrowserResponse:
        async with self._lock:
            self._restarted = False
            if action == "stop":
                await self._stop()
            elif action == "restart":
                await self._stop()
                await self._start()
                self._restarted = True
            elif action == "start":
                await self._start()
            elif action != "health":
                raise BrowserError("INVALID_ACTION", f"Unknown session action: {action}")
            return await self.describe(f"session_{action}", evidence={"running": self.running})

    async def open(self, url: str, *, new_tab=False, wait_for="load"):
        self.policy.check("open")
        self.validate_url(url)
        async with self.operation() as page:
            return await self._open(page, url, new_tab=new_tab, wait_for=wait_for)

    async def _open(self, page, url: str, *, new_tab=False, wait_for="load"):
        self.validate_url(url)
        if url != "about:blank":
            await self.policy.resolve(url)
        if new_tab:
            if self.tabs.count >= self.max_tabs:
                raise BrowserError("TAB_LIMIT", "Browser tab limit reached")
            page = await self._context.new_page()
        try:
            response = await page.goto(url, wait_until=wait_for, timeout=30000)
        except PlaywrightError:
            if self._navigation_error:
                raise self._navigation_error from None
            raise
        if page.url != "about:blank":
            await self.policy.resolve(page.url)
        result = await self.describe("open", evidence={
            "requested_url": url, "final_url": page.url,
            "status": response.status if response else None,
            "host_matches": urlsplit(url).hostname == urlsplit(page.url).hostname,
        })
        if response and response.status >= 400:
            result.success = False
            result.error_code = "HTTP_ERROR"
            result.error = f"Navigation returned HTTP {response.status}"
        return result

    async def tab_action(self, action: str, *, tab=None, url=None):
        self.policy.check("tabs")
        async with self.operation() as page:
            return await self._tab_action(page, action, tab=tab, url=url)

    async def _tab_action(self, page, action: str, *, tab=None, url=None):
        if url is not None:
            self.validate_url(url)
        if action == "new":
            if self.tabs.count >= self.max_tabs:
                raise BrowserError("TAB_LIMIT", "Browser tab limit reached")
            page = await self._context.new_page()
            if url:
                await self._open(page, url)
        elif action == "switch":
            page = self.tabs.page(tab)
            await page.bring_to_front()
            self.tabs.active = tab
        elif action == "close":
            await self.tabs.page(tab).close()
            if self.tabs.active is not None:
                await self.tabs.page().bring_to_front()
        elif action in {"back", "forward", "reload"}:
            if tab is not None:
                page = self.tabs.page(tab)
                await page.bring_to_front()
                self.tabs.active = tab
            method = {"back": page.go_back, "forward": page.go_forward, "reload": page.reload}[action]
            await method(timeout=30000)
        elif action != "list":
            raise BrowserError("INVALID_ACTION", f"Unknown tab action: {action}")
        return await self.describe(f"tabs_{action}", evidence={"active_tab": self.tabs.active})


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\state.py

"""Bounded page-state evidence. A fingerprint is not a task-completion verdict."""

import hashlib
import json

from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError

CAPTURE = """() => {
        const root = document.scrollingElement || document.documentElement;
        const hash = text => {
            let h = 2166136261;
            for (let i=0; i<text.length; i++) h = Math.imul(h ^ text.charCodeAt(i), 16777619);
            return (h >>> 0).toString(16);
        };
        const roots = [document];
        // Include open shadow trees while bounding traversal and evidence size.
        for (let index=0; index<roots.length && roots.length<32; index++) {
            for (const el of Array.from(roots[index].querySelectorAll('*')).slice(0,12000)) {
                if (el.shadowRoot && roots.length<32) roots.push(el.shadowRoot);
            }
        }
        const controls = roots.flatMap(root => Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select'))).slice(0,200)
            .map(el => [el.type, el.type === 'password' || /^(cc-|transaction-)/i.test(el.autocomplete || '') ? '' : el.value.slice(0,100), el.checked]);
        const shadows = roots.slice(1).map(root => root.textContent.slice(0,2000)).join('');
        const scrolls = roots.flatMap(root => Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('*')).slice(0,12000))
            .filter(el => el.scrollTop || el.scrollLeft).slice(0,100).map(el => [el.scrollLeft,el.scrollTop]);
        return {url:location.href, title:document.title, x:scrollX, y:scrollY,
            height:innerHeight, width:innerWidth, scroll_height:root.scrollHeight,
            at_top:scrollY <= 1, at_bottom:scrollY + innerHeight >= root.scrollHeight - 1,
            dom:hash((document.body?.innerText || '').slice(0,30000) + shadows + JSON.stringify(controls) + JSON.stringify(scrolls))};
    }"""


async def capture_state(page) -> dict:
    data = await page.evaluate(CAPTURE)
    frames = []
    for frame in page.frames[1:16]:
        try:
            frames.append(await frame.evaluate(CAPTURE))
        except PlaywrightError:
            frames.append({"url": frame.url, "unavailable": True})
    data["frames"] = frames
    data["fingerprint"] = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
    return data


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\tabs.py

"""Stable IDs are monotonic even across Chromium generations."""

from typing import Any

from playwright.async_api import Error as PlaywrightError

from .errors import BrowserError
from .schemas import TabInfo


class TabRegistry:
    def __init__(self):
        self._pages: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._next_id = 1
        self.active: str | None = None

    @property
    def count(self):
        return len(self._pages)

    def register(self, page) -> str:
        for tab_id, existing in self._pages.items():
            if existing is page:
                return tab_id
        tab_id = f"t{self._next_id}"
        self._next_id += 1
        self._pages[tab_id] = page
        self.active = tab_id
        page.on("close", lambda *_: self.remove(tab_id))
        return tab_id

    def remove(self, tab_id: str):
        self._pages.pop(tab_id, None)
        if self.active == tab_id:
            self.active = next(reversed(self._pages), None)

    def clear(self):
        self._pages.clear()
        self.active = None

    def page(self, tab_id: str | None = None):
        target = tab_id if tab_id is not None else self.active
        page = self._pages.get(target)
        if page is None or page.is_closed():
            raise BrowserError("TAB_NOT_FOUND", f"Unknown or closed tab: {target}")
        return page

    async def snapshot(self) -> list[TabInfo]:
        result = []
        for tab_id, page in list(self._pages.items()):
            if page.is_closed():
                self.remove(tab_id)
                continue
            try:
                title = await page.title()
            except PlaywrightError:
                title = ""  # A navigating popup may not yet have a document.
            result.append(TabInfo(tab_id=tab_id, url=page.url, title=title, active=tab_id == self.active))
        return result


================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\xbrowser\token_budget.py
================================================================================

"""Actual Qwen token counting when configured; explicitly labelled fallback."""

import hashlib
import json
import os
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path


@lru_cache(maxsize=2)
def load_tokenizer(path):
    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
    expected = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name("tokenizer_config.json").read_text())["sha256"]
    if hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest() != expected:
        raise ValueError("Configured Qwen tokenizer checksum does not match tokenizer_config.json")
    return Tokenizer.from_file(path)


def tokenizer():
    path = os.environ.get("XBROWSER_TOKENIZER_PATH")
    if path:
        return load_tokenizer(str(Path(path).resolve()))
    cached = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / ".cache" / "xbrowser" / "tokenizer.json"
    return load_tokenizer(str(cached)) if cached.exists() else None


def token_count(text):
    encoder = tokenizer()
    return len(encoder.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids) if encoder else None


def main():
    import argparse
    import urllib.request
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Cache the checksum-pinned official Qwen tokenizer for local development")
    parser.add_argument("--download", action="store_true", required=True)
    parser.parse_args()
    config = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name("tokenizer_config.json").read_text())
    data = urllib.request.urlopen(config["url"], timeout=120).read()
    if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != config["sha256"]:
        raise ValueError("Downloaded tokenizer checksum mismatch")
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / ".cache" / "xbrowser" / "tokenizer.json"
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_bytes(data)
    print(f"Cached verified tokenizer at {path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Deterministic acceptance checks, separate from model-visible action tools."""

import hashlib

from .errors import BrowserError
from .schemas import ArtifactCheck


async def verify(session, request):
    results = []
    async with session.operation() as page:
        for check in request.checks:
            passed = False
            error = None
            try:
                if isinstance(check, ArtifactCheck):
                    path = session.artifact_path(check.path)
                    if not path.is_file():
                        raise BrowserError("MISSING_ARTIFACT", "Required artifact does not exist")
                    if path.stat().st_size > 50 * 1024 * 1024:
                        raise BrowserError("ARTIFACT_LIMIT", "Acceptance artifact exceeds the 50 MiB verification limit")
                    content = path.read_bytes()
                    passed = len(content) >= check.min_bytes
                    if check.contains is not None:
                        passed = passed and check.contains in content.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
                    if check.sha256:
                        passed = passed and hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest() == check.sha256
                    if check.png or check.width is not None or check.height is not None:
                        from PIL import Image
                        with Image.open(path) as picture:
                            passed = passed and picture.format == "PNG" and picture.width > 0 and picture.height > 0
                            if check.width is not None:
                                passed = passed and picture.width == check.width
                            if check.height is not None:
                                passed = passed and picture.height == check.height
                            picture.verify()
                elif check.kind == "url":
                    passed = page.url == check.expected
                elif check.kind == "text":
                    passed = check.expected in await page.locator("body").inner_text(timeout=5000)
                elif check.kind == "tab_count":
                    passed = len(await session.tabs.snapshot()) == int(check.expected)
            except (BrowserError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
                passed = False
                error = str(exc)
            results.append({"check": check.model_dump(), "passed": bool(passed), "error": error})
        result = await session.describe("verify")
        result.success = all(item["passed"] for item in results)
        result.task_verification = "passed" if result.success else "failed"
        result.data = {"checks": results}
        result.output = "\n".join(f"{i+1}. {item['check']['kind']}: {'passed' if item['passed'] else 'failed'}" for i, item in enumerate(results))
        if not result.success:
            result.error = "One or more trusted acceptance checks failed"
            result.error_code = "TASK_INCOMPLETE"
        return result

"""Tests for browser automation tools — schema validation and sandbox-absent fallback."""

import pytest

from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionTier
from local_agent.tools.browser import (
    BrowserClickTool,
    BrowserGetContentTool,
    BrowserNavigateTool,
    BrowserObserveTool,
    BrowserScreenshotTool,
    BrowserTypeTool,
)

# ── Schema and metadata tests (sync, no sandbox needed) ─────────────────

class TestBrowserToolSchemas:
    """Verify all browser tools have correct schemas and metadata."""

    TOOLS = (
        ("browser_navigate", BrowserNavigateTool),
        ("browser_observe", BrowserObserveTool),
        ("browser_get_content", BrowserGetContentTool),
        ("browser_click", BrowserClickTool),
        ("browser_type", BrowserTypeTool),
        ("browser_screenshot", BrowserScreenshotTool),
    )

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("expected_name,tool_cls", TOOLS)
    def test_tool_name(self, expected_name, tool_cls):
        tool = tool_cls()
        assert tool.name == expected_name

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("expected_name,tool_cls", TOOLS)
    def test_tool_description_non_empty(self, expected_name, tool_cls):
        tool = tool_cls()
        assert len(tool.description) > 10

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("expected_name,tool_cls", TOOLS)
    def test_tool_permission_tier(self, expected_name, tool_cls):
        tool = tool_cls()
        assert tool.permission_tier == PermissionTier.NETWORK

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("expected_name,tool_cls", TOOLS)
    def test_openai_format(self, expected_name, tool_cls):
        tool = tool_cls()
        defn = tool.to_openai_tool()
        assert defn["type"] == "function"
        assert defn["function"]["name"] == expected_name
        assert "parameters" in defn["function"]
        assert defn["function"]["parameters"]["type"] == "object"

    def test_navigate_requires_url(self):
        tool = BrowserNavigateTool()
        schema = tool.parameters_schema
        assert "url" in schema["properties"]
        assert "url" in schema["required"]

    def test_type_requires_text_and_a_target(self):
        tool = BrowserTypeTool()
        schema = tool.parameters_schema
        assert "selector" in schema["properties"]
        assert "text" in schema["properties"]
        assert {"required": ["selector"]} in schema["anyOf"]
        assert {"required": ["ref", "snapshot_id"]} in schema["anyOf"]
        assert "text" in schema["required"]

    def test_get_content_has_mode_enum(self):
        tool = BrowserGetContentTool()
        schema = tool.parameters_schema
        assert "mode" in schema["properties"]
        assert set(schema["properties"]["mode"]["enum"]) == {"a11y", "text", "html"}

        assert verification.status == VerificationStatus.FAILED

        tool = BrowserClickTool()
        result = ToolResult(success=True, output="Clicked")
        verification = await tool.verify({"text": "Submit"}, result)
        assert verification.status == VerificationStatus.PASSED

"""Tests for the configuration system."""

import os
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

from local_agent.config import AgentConfig, ModelRoleConfig


class TestAgentConfig(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_default_config(self):
        cfg = AgentConfig()
        self.assertEqual(cfg.ollama_base_url, "http://localhost:11434")
        self.assertEqual(cfg.sandbox_url, "http://localhost:9000")
        self.assertEqual(cfg.max_steps, 50)
        self.assertIn("planner", cfg.model_roles)

    def test_ensure_dirs_creates_directories(self):
        import tempfile
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            cfg = AgentConfig(
                workspace_dir=Path(tmp) / "ws",
                db_path=Path(tmp) / "data" / "test.db",
                trace_dir=Path(tmp) / "traces",
            )
            cfg.ensure_dirs()
            self.assertTrue(cfg.workspace_dir.exists())
            self.assertTrue(cfg.db_path.parent.exists())
            self.assertTrue(cfg.trace_dir.exists())

    def test_from_env(self):
        os.environ["XALGV_MAX_STEPS"] = "99"
        try:
            cfg = AgentConfig.from_env()
            self.assertEqual(cfg.max_steps, 99)
        finally:
            del os.environ["XALGV_MAX_STEPS"]

    def test_model_role_config(self):
        role = ModelRoleConfig(model="qwen3:8b", context_length=8192, temperature=0.6, thinking=True)
        self.assertEqual(role.model, "qwen3:8b")
        self.assertTrue(role.thinking)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
