import pytest

from sandbox.xbrowser import actions
from sandbox.xbrowser.errors import BrowserError
from sandbox.xbrowser.observe import observe
from sandbox.xbrowser.schemas import ClickRequest, ObserveRequest, TypeRequest


async def snapshot(browser, page, **kwargs):
    return await observe(browser, page, ObserveRequest(**kwargs))


def element(result, name, *, frame=None):
    return next(item for item in result.data["elements"] if item["name"] == name and
                (frame is None or item["frame_id"] == frame))


def click_request(result, target):
    return ClickRequest(ref=target["ref"], snapshot_id=result.snapshot_id)


async def test_discovers_visible_controls_without_mutating_dom(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        await page.evaluate("""() => {
            window.mutations=[];
            new MutationObserver(records=>window.mutations.push(...records))
                .observe(document,{subtree:true,attributes:true,childList:true});
        }""")
        result = await snapshot(browser, page)
        names = [item["name"] for item in result.data["elements"]]
        assert "Display name" in names and "Save profile" in names
        assert "Shadow action" in names and "Shadow name" in names
        assert "Covered action" not in names and "Hidden action" not in names
        assert "Below viewport" not in names
        assert element(result, "Disabled action")["disabled"]
        assert result.data["counts"]["below"] > 0
        assert await page.evaluate("window.mutations.length") == 0
        assert "secret-do-not-expose" not in result.model_dump_json()
        assert "hidden-secret" not in result.model_dump_json()


async def test_reference_click_type_and_unrelated_updates(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        result = await snapshot(browser, page)
        await page.locator("#clock").evaluate("el=>el.textContent='12:01'")
        field = element(result, "Display name")
        typed = await actions.type_text(browser, page, TypeRequest(
            ref=field["ref"], snapshot_id=result.snapshot_id, text="Ada",
        ))
        assert typed.success and typed.action_verification["value_matches"]
        assert typed.state_changed
        clicked = await actions.click(browser, page, click_request(result, element(result, "Save profile")))
        assert clicked.success and clicked.state_changed
        assert await page.locator("#result").inner_text() == "Saved"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", ["replace", "rename", "href", "hide"])
async def test_changed_target_is_rejected_before_dispatch(browser, site, change):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        result = await snapshot(browser, page)
        target = element(result, "Dynamic action")
        scripts = {
            "replace": "el=>el.replaceWith(el.cloneNode(true))",
            "rename": "el=>el.textContent='Different action'",
            "href": "el=>el.setAttribute('href','https://example.com/different')",
            "hide": "el=>el.style.display='none'",
        }
        await page.locator("#dynamic").evaluate(scripts[change])
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
            await actions.click(browser, page, click_request(result, target))
        assert error.value.code == "STALE_REF"
        assert await page.evaluate("window.clicked") is None


async def test_old_snapshot_tab_and_navigation_are_rejected(browser, site):
    first = await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        old = await snapshot(browser, page)
        current = await snapshot(browser, page)
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
            await browser.references.resolve(browser, old.snapshot_id, 1)
        assert error.value.code == "STALE_REF"
    second = await browser.tab_action("new", url=f"{site}/marks.html")
    assert second.tab_id != first.tab_id
    with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
        await browser.references.resolve(browser, current.snapshot_id, 1)
    assert error.value.code == "STALE_REF"
    await browser.tab_action("switch", tab=first.tab_id)
    await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")  # Reload with the same URL also invalidates references.
    with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
        await browser.references.resolve(browser, current.snapshot_id, 1)
    assert error.value.code == "STALE_REF"


async def test_shadow_and_cross_origin_frame_references(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        result = await snapshot(browser, page)
        await actions.click(browser, page, click_request(result, element(result, "Shadow action")))
        assert await page.locator("#shadow").get_by_text("Shadow clicked").count() == 1
        await actions.type_text(browser, page, TypeRequest(
            ref=element(result, "Shadow name")["ref"], snapshot_id=result.snapshot_id, text="Shadow value",
        ))
        assert await page.locator("#shadow").locator("input").input_value() == "Shadow value"
        frame_actions = [item for item in result.data["elements"] if item["name"] == "Frame action"]
        assert len(frame_actions) == 2
        for target in frame_actions:
            await actions.type_text(browser, page, TypeRequest(
                ref=element(result, "Frame name", frame=target["frame_id"])["ref"],
                snapshot_id=result.snapshot_id, text="Frame value",
            ))
            await actions.click(browser, page, click_request(result, target))
        for frame in page.frames[1:]:
            assert await frame.get_by_text("Frame clicked").count() == 1
            assert await frame.locator("input").input_value() == "Frame value"


async def test_disabled_refs_are_not_actionable(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        result = await snapshot(browser, page)
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
            await actions.click(browser, page, click_request(result, element(result, "Disabled action")))
        assert error.value.code == "TARGET_NOT_ACTIONABLE"


async def test_page_and_frame_occlusion_rechecked_before_action(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        result = await snapshot(browser, page)
        target = element(result, "Frame action", frame="f2")
        await page.locator("iframe").first.evaluate("""el => {
            const rect=el.getBoundingClientRect(), cover=document.createElement('div');
            cover.style=`position:fixed;left:${rect.x}px;top:${rect.y}px;width:${rect.width}px;height:${rect.height}px;z-index:999`;
            document.body.append(cover);
        }""")
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
            await actions.click(browser, page, click_request(result, target))
        assert error.value.code == "TARGET_NOT_ACTIONABLE"
        await page.evaluate("""() => {
            const rect=document.querySelector('#save').getBoundingClientRect();
            const cover=document.createElement('div');
            cover.style=`position:fixed;left:${rect.x}px;top:${rect.y}px;width:${rect.width}px;height:${rect.height}px;z-index:999`;
            document.body.append(cover);
        }""")
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
            await actions.click(browser, page, click_request(result, element(result, "Save profile")))
        assert error.value.code == "TARGET_NOT_ACTIONABLE"


================================================================================
FILE: tests\xbrowser\test_observe.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from sandbox.xbrowser.errors import BrowserError
from sandbox.xbrowser.observe import observe
from sandbox.xbrowser.schemas import ObserveRequest


@pytest.mark.parametrize("budget", [800, 1600, 2400])
async def test_observation_is_bounded_and_only_reported_refs_resolve(browser, budget):
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        await page.set_content("<title>Large page</title>" + "".join(
            f'<button style="display:block">Action {i} ' + "x" * 100 + "</button>" for i in range(200)
        ))
        result = await observe(browser, page, ObserveRequest(scope="page", max_chars=budget, text=True))
        assert len(result.output) <= budget
        assert result.data["output_chars"] == len(result.output)
        assert result.data["counts"]["omitted"] > 0
        assert result.state_after and result.snapshot_id in result.output
        for item in result.data["elements"]:
            assert f"[{item['ref']}]" in result.output
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
            await browser.references.resolve(browser, result.snapshot_id, len(result.data["elements"]) + 1)
        assert error.value.code == "INVALID_REF"


async def test_page_scope_exposes_below_viewport_and_scroll_state(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/marks.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        result = await observe(browser, page, ObserveRequest(scope="page", max_elements=100, max_chars=8000))
        below = next(item for item in result.data["elements"] if item["name"] == "Below viewport")
        target = await browser.references.resolve(browser, result.snapshot_id, below["ref"])
        await target.click(timeout=5000)  # Playwright scrolls the exact retained element into view.
        after = await observe(browser, page, ObserveRequest())
        assert after.state_after != result.state_after
        assert after.data["scroll"]["y"] > 0


async def test_snapshot_identity_changes_after_restart(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        old = await observe(browser, page, ObserveRequest())
    await browser.control("restart")
    await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        current = await observe(browser, page, ObserveRequest())
        assert current.snapshot_id != old.snapshot_id
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
            await browser.references.resolve(browser, old.snapshot_id, 1)
        assert error.value.code == "STALE_REF"


async def test_multilingual_observation_respects_measured_token_budget(browser):
    from sandbox.xbrowser.token_budget import token_count
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        await page.set_content("<title>多语言页面</title>" + "".join(
            f'<button style="display:block">操作 {i} 中文 العربية हिन्दी ' + "界" * 50 + "</button>" for i in range(100)
        ))
        result = await observe(browser, page, ObserveRequest(scope="page", max_elements=100, max_chars=8000, text=True))
        actual = token_count(result.output)
        if actual is not None:
            assert actual == result.data["token_count"] <= 700
        else:
            assert result.data["tokenizer"] == "character_estimate"
        for item in result.data["elements"]:
            assert f"[{item['ref']}]" in result.output


================================================================================
FILE: tests\xbrowser\test_security.py
================================================================================

import asyncio
import socket
from urllib.parse import quote

import pytest

from sandbox.xbrowser.controller import execute
from sandbox.xbrowser.errors import BrowserError
from sandbox.xbrowser.safety import BrowserPolicy
from sandbox.xbrowser.schemas import ClickRequest, OpenRequest, PressRequest, TypeRequest


@pytest.mark.parametrize("url", ["http://127.0.0.1", "http://[::1]", "http://169.254.169.254", "http://10.0.0.1",
                                  "http://2130706433", "http://0x7f000001", "http://[::ffff:127.0.0.1]",
                                  "http://[64:ff9b::a00:1]", "http://224.0.0.1"])
async def test_default_policy_blocks_private_metadata_and_encoded_ips(url):
    with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as error:
        await BrowserPolicy().resolve(url)
    assert error.value.code == "NAVIGATION_DENIED"


async def test_mixed_dns_answers_fail_closed(monkeypatch):
    async def addresses(*args, **kwargs):
        return [(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, "", (ip, 80)) for ip in ("93.184.215.14", "127.0.0.1")]
    monkeypatch.setattr(asyncio.get_running_loop(), "getaddrinfo", addresses)
    with pytest.raises(BrowserError):
        await BrowserPolicy().resolve("http://public.example")


def test_domain_policy_uses_exact_and_explicit_subdomain_rules():
    policy = BrowserPolicy(allowed_domains=("example.com", "*.example.org"), denied_domains=("bad.example.org",))
    assert policy.destination("https://example.com")[0] == "example.com"
    assert policy.destination("https://good.example.org")[0] == "good.example.org"
    for url in ["https://example.com.attacker.test", "https://bad.example.org", "https://x.example.com"]:
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError):
            policy.destination(url)


async def test_redirect_and_subresource_cannot_bypass_private_network_policy(browser, site):
    result = await execute(browser, "open", OpenRequest(url=f"{site}/redirect?target={quote('http://127.0.0.1/private', safe='')}"))
    assert not result.success
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        await page.goto(f"{site}/first.html")
        blocked = await page.evaluate("""async () => {
            try { await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:1/private'); return false; } catch { return true; }
        }""")
        assert blocked


async def test_sensitive_actions_fail_closed_and_single_use_approval_is_scoped(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/sensitive.html")
    request = TypeRequest(selector="#password", text="do-not-echo")
    denied = await execute(browser, "type", request)
    assert denied.error_code == "HUMAN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED"
    assert "do-not-echo" not in denied.model_dump_json()
    async with browser.operation():
        token = await browser.approvals.issue(browser, "type", request)
    approved = await execute(browser, "type", request, approval_token=token)
    assert approved.success
    replay = await execute(browser, "type", request, approval_token=token)
    assert replay.error_code == "APPROVAL_INVALID"
    click = await execute(browser, "click", ClickRequest(selector="#submit"))
    assert click.error_code == "HUMAN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED"
    key = await execute(browser, "press", PressRequest(key="Enter"))
    assert key.error_code == "HUMAN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED"
    async with browser.operation():
        token = await browser.approvals.issue(browser, "type", request)
    altered = await execute(browser, "type", TypeRequest(selector="#password", text="changed"), approval_token=token)
    assert altered.error_code == "APPROVAL_INVALID"


async def test_scripted_network_write_is_blocked_without_approval(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        assert await page.evaluate("""async () => {
            try { await fetch('/submitted', {method:'POST',body:'write'}); return false; } catch { return true; }
        }""")
    assert any(event.get("code") == "HUMAN_APPROVAL_REQUIRED" for event in browser.events)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("key", ["Control+L", "Control+O", "Control+Shift+J", "F12", "Alt+F4", "Control+S"])
async def test_browser_chrome_and_developer_shortcuts_are_disabled(browser, key):
    result = await execute(browser, "press", PressRequest(key=key))
    assert not result.success and result.error_code == "KEY_DENIED"


async def test_file_api_cannot_read_process_environment_or_profiles(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from sandbox import tool_server
    monkeypatch.setattr(tool_server, "WORKSPACE", tmp_path)
    outside = tmp_path.parent / "outside-secret.txt"
    outside.write_text("administrative secret")
    profile = tmp_path / ".xbrowser" / "profile" / "cookies.txt"
    profile.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    profile.write_text("private cookies")
    for path in (outside, profile):
        result = await tool_server.read_file(tool_server.ReadFileRequest(path=str(path)))
        assert not result.success
    allowed = tmp_path / "report.md"
    allowed.write_text("public report")
    assert (await tool_server.read_file(tool_server.ReadFileRequest(path=str(allowed)))).success


async def test_webrtc_and_file_uploads_are_disabled(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/sensitive.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        assert await page.evaluate("typeof RTCPeerConnection") == "undefined"
    result = await execute(browser, "click", ClickRequest(selector="#upload"))
    assert not result.success and result.error_code == "UPLOAD_DISABLED"

FILE: tests\xbrowser\test_session.py

import asyncio

import pytest

from sandbox.xbrowser import BrowserSession
from sandbox.xbrowser.errors import BrowserError


async def test_lifecycle_and_persistent_storage(browser, site):
    health = await browser.control("health")
    assert not health.data["running"]
    assert health.generation == 0  # Health checks never launch Chromium.
    first = await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    assert first.success and first.generation == 1
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        await page.evaluate("localStorage.setItem('persisted', 'yes')")
    assert (await browser.control("start")).generation == 1
    await browser.control("stop")
    second = await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    assert second.generation == 2
    assert second.tab_id != first.tab_id
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        assert await page.evaluate("localStorage.getItem('persisted')") == "yes"


async def test_unexpected_context_close_recovers_without_reusing_ids(browser, site):
    original = await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    await browser._context.close()
    restored = await browser.tab_action("list")
    assert restored.restarted
    assert restored.generation == original.generation + 1
    assert restored.tab_id != original.tab_id
    with pytest.raises(BrowserError, match="Unknown or closed tab"):
        await browser.tab_action("switch", tab=original.tab_id)


async def test_explicit_restart(browser):
    original = await browser.control("start")
    restarted = await browser.control("restart")
    assert restarted.restarted
    assert restarted.session_id == original.session_id
    assert restarted.generation == original.generation + 1
    assert restarted.tab_id != original.tab_id


async def test_operations_are_serialized_and_cancellation_releases_lock(browser):
    entered = asyncio.Event()
    release = asyncio.Event()

    async def hold():
        async with browser.operation():
            entered.set()
            await release.wait()

    holder = asyncio.create_task(hold())
    await asyncio.wait_for(entered.wait(), 30)
    waiter = asyncio.create_task(browser.control("stop"))
    await asyncio.sleep(0)
    assert not waiter.done()
    holder.cancel()
    with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
        await holder
    await asyncio.wait_for(waiter, 10)
    assert not browser.running


async def test_profile_scopes_are_isolated(browser, tmp_path, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        await page.evaluate("localStorage.setItem('private', 'secret')")
    other = BrowserSession(tmp_path, scope="other", headless=True)
    other.policy = browser.policy
    try:
        await other.open(f"{site}/first.html")
        async with other.operation() as page:
            assert await page.evaluate("localStorage.getItem('private')") is None
    finally:
        await other.control("stop")


async def test_screenshots_are_unique_and_never_overwritten(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        first = await browser.capture(page)
        second = await browser.capture(page)
        assert first != second
        assert first.read_bytes().startswith(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")
        before = first.read_bytes()
        with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as exc:
            await browser.capture(page, path=str(first))
        assert exc.value.code == "ARTIFACT_EXISTS"
        assert first.read_bytes() == before


async def test_failed_capture_removes_partial_artifact(browser):
    class FailedPage:
        async def screenshot(self, **kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("capture interrupted")

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="capture interrupted"):
        await browser.capture(FailedPage(), path="incomplete.png")
    assert not (browser.workspace / "incomplete.png").exists()


async def test_crash_notification_triggers_new_generation(browser):
    original = await browser.control("start")
    browser._on_crash()  # Inject the renderer event without hanging a CDP Page.crash call.
    recovered = await browser.tab_action("list")
    assert recovered.restarted and recovered.generation == original.generation + 1
    assert recovered.tab_id != original.tab_id


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["../escape.png", ".xbrowser/leak.png", "capture.jpg"])
def test_screenshot_paths_confined(tmp_path, path):
    with pytest.raises(BrowserError):
        BrowserSession(tmp_path).screenshot_target(path)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("url", ["file:///etc/passwd", "javascript:alert(1)", "data:text/html,x", "https://u:p@host/"])
def test_unsafe_schemes_and_credentials_rejected(url):
    with pytest.raises(BrowserError):
        BrowserSession.validate_url(url)


def test_invalid_scope_rejected(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        BrowserSession(tmp_path, scope="../other")

FILE: tests\xbrowser\test_tabs.py

import asyncio

import pytest

from sandbox.xbrowser.errors import BrowserError
from sandbox.xbrowser.tabs import TabRegistry


async def test_switch_close_and_active_url(browser, site):
    first = await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    second = await browser.tab_action("new", url=f"{site}/second.html")
    assert first.tab_id != second.tab_id
    assert len(second.data["tabs"]) == 2
    switched = await browser.tab_action("switch", tab=first.tab_id)
    assert switched.tab_id == first.tab_id
    active = next(tab for tab in switched.data["tabs"] if tab["active"])
    assert active["url"] == f"{site}/first.html"
    assert active["title"] == "First fixture"
    closed = await browser.tab_action("close", tab=first.tab_id)
    assert closed.tab_id == second.tab_id
    assert len(closed.data["tabs"]) == 1
    await browser.tab_action("close", tab=second.tab_id)
    reopened = await browser.tab_action("list")
    assert reopened.tab_id not in {first.tab_id, second.tab_id}


async def test_popup_registration_and_external_close(browser, site):
    first = await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        async with page.expect_popup() as popup_event:
            await page.get_by_text("Open second tab").click()
        popup = await popup_event.value
        await popup.wait_for_load_state()
    state = await browser.tab_action("list")
    assert len(state.data["tabs"]) == 2
    assert state.tab_id != first.tab_id
    await popup.close()
    state = await browser.tab_action("list")
    assert state.tab_id == first.tab_id


async def test_invalid_tab_does_not_change_active(browser):
    original = await browser.control("start")
    with pytest.raises(BrowserError) as exc:
        await browser.tab_action("switch", tab="t999")
    assert exc.value.code == "TAB_NOT_FOUND"
    assert browser.tabs.active == original.tab_id


async def test_concurrent_new_tabs_get_distinct_ids(browser, site):
    await browser.control("start")
    results = await asyncio.gather(*[
        browser.tab_action("new", url=f"{site}/second.html") for _ in range(3)
    ])
    assert len({result.tab_id for result in results}) == 3
    assert len((await browser.tab_action("list")).data["tabs"]) == 4


async def test_history_navigation(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    await browser.open(f"{site}/second.html")
    back = await browser.tab_action("back")
    assert back.data["tabs"][0]["url"].endswith("first.html")
    forward = await browser.tab_action("forward")
    assert forward.data["tabs"][0]["url"].endswith("second.html")
    assert (await browser.tab_action("reload")).data["tabs"][0]["title"] == "Second fixture"


async def test_confirmation_is_dismissed(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    async with browser.operation() as page:
        await page.get_by_role("button", name="Confirm").click()
        assert await page.locator("#answer").inner_text() == "dismissed"
    assert browser.events[-1]["kind"] == "confirm"


def test_registry_registration_is_idempotent_and_ids_survive_clear():
    class Page:
        def on(self, *args):
            pass

    registry = TabRegistry()
    page = Page()
    assert registry.register(page) == registry.register(page) == "t1"
    registry.clear()
    assert registry.register(page) == "t2"

FILE: tests\xbrowser\test_verification.py

from pathlib import Path

from sandbox.xbrowser.controller import execute
from sandbox.xbrowser.schemas import ScreenshotRequest, VerificationRequest
from sandbox.xbrowser.verification import verify


async def test_task_checks_require_all_artifacts_and_contents(browser, site):
    await browser.open(f"{site}/first.html")
    screenshot = await execute(browser, "screenshot", ScreenshotRequest(path="one.png", marks=True))
    assert screenshot.success and screenshot.snapshot_id and screenshot.data["elements"]
    assert Path(screenshot.artifacts[0]).read_bytes().startswith(b"\x89PNG")
    checks = [{"kind": "artifact", "path": "one.png", "png": True},
              {"kind": "artifact", "path": "missing.png", "png": True},
              {"kind": "url", "expected": f"{site}/first.html"},
              {"kind": "text", "expected": "XBrowser first page"}]
    failed = await verify(browser, VerificationRequest(checks=checks))
    assert not failed.success and failed.task_verification == "failed"
    assert failed.data["checks"][0]["passed"] and not failed.data["checks"][1]["passed"]
    (browser.workspace / "missing.png").write_bytes(Path(screenshot.artifacts[0]).read_bytes())
    passed = await verify(browser, VerificationRequest(checks=checks))
    assert passed.success and passed.task_verification == "passed"
    dimension_failure = await verify(browser, VerificationRequest(checks=[
        {"kind": "artifact", "path": "one.png", "width": 1, "height": 1},
    ]))
    assert not dimension_failure.success
    (browser.workspace / "fake.png").write_text("not an image")
    corrupt = await verify(browser, VerificationRequest(checks=[{"kind": "artifact", "path": "fake.png", "png": True}]))
    assert not corrupt.success


async def test_task_checks_confine_paths_and_verify_report_contents(browser):
    (browser.workspace / "report.md").write_text("All five sources were recorded.")
    result = await verify(browser, VerificationRequest(checks=[
        {"kind": "artifact", "path": "report.md", "contains": "five sources"},
        {"kind": "artifact", "path": "../escape.txt"},
        {"kind": "artifact", "path": ".xbrowser/test/profile/secret"},
    ]))
    assert not result.success
    assert [item["passed"] for item in result.data["checks"]] == [True, False, False]

                    <!-- Devices will be populated by JavaScript -->
                </tbody>

    <script>
        // Device data
        const devices = [
            { hostname: 'Gateway', ip: '192.168.1.1', status: 'Online' },
            { hostname: 'Workstation-01', ip: '192.168.1.10', status: 'Online' },
            { hostname: 'Laptop-02', ip: '192.168.1.11', status: 'Online' },
            { hostname: 'Printer', ip: '192.168.1.20', status: 'Offline' },
            { hostname: 'NAS', ip: '192.168.1.30', status: 'Online' },
            { hostname: 'Camera', ip: '192.168.1.40', status: 'Offline' }
        ];

        // DOM Elements
        const deviceList = document.getElementById('device-list');
        const totalDevicesEl = document.getElementById('total-devices');
        const onlineDevicesEl = document.getElementById('online-devices');
        const offlineDevicesEl = document.getElementById('offline-devices');
        const searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input');
        const clearSearchBtn = document.getElementById('clear-search');
        const filterButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn');

        // Initialize dashboard
        function initDashboard() {
            renderDevices(devices);
            updateSummaryCards();
        }

        // Render devices to table
        function renderDevices(devicesToRender) {
            deviceList.innerHTML = '';
            
            devicesToRender.forEach(device => {
                const statusClass = device.status === 'Online' ? 'status-online' : 'status-offline';
                const statusText = device.status === 'Online' ? 'Online' : 'Offline';
                
                const row = document.createElement('tr');
                row.innerHTML = `
                    <td><strong>${device.hostname}</strong></td>
                    <td>${device.ip}</td>
                    <td><span class="status-badge ${statusClass}">${statusText}</span></td>
                `;
                deviceList.appendChild(row);
            });
        }

        // Update summary cards
        function updateSummaryCards() {
            const total = devices.length;
            const online = devices.filter(d => d.status === 'Online').length;
            const offline = devices.filter(d => d.status === 'Offline').length;

            totalDevicesEl.textContent = total;
            onlineDevicesEl.textContent = online;
            offlineDevicesEl.textContent = offline;
        }

        // Filter by status
        function filterByStatus(filterType) {
            if (filterType === 'all') {
                renderDevices(devices);
            } else if (filterType === 'online') {
                renderDevices(devices.filter(d => d.status === 'Online'));
            } else if (filterType === 'offline') {
                renderDevices(devices.filter(d => d.status === 'Offline'));
            }
        }

        // Search functionality
        function filterBySearch(searchTerm) {
            const lowerTerm = searchTerm.toLowerCase();
            const filtered = devices.filter(device => 
                device.hostname.toLowerCase().includes(lowerTerm) ||
                device.ip.includes(lowerTerm)
            );
            renderDevices(filtered);
        }

        // Event Listeners
        searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            filterBySearch(e.target.value);
        });

        clearSearchBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            searchInput.value = '';
            filterByStatus('all');
        });

        filterButtons.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                filterButtons.forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
                btn.classList.add('active');
                filterByStatus(btn.dataset.filter);
            });
        });

        // Initialize
        initDashboard();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Script to generate a professionally formatted DOCX research report on Rabindranath Tagore.
Uses python-docx to create the document with proper formatting, headers, footers, and references.
"""

from docx import Document
from docx.shared import Pt, Inches
from docx.enum.text import WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH
import re
from docx.oxml.ns import qn
from docx.oxml import OxmlElement

# Read the research.md file
with open('/workspace/poet_report/research.md', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

# Create a new Document
doc = Document()

# Title and subtitle
title = doc.add_heading('Rabindranath Tagore: A Research Profile', 0)
title.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER

subtitle = doc.add_paragraph('A Comprehensive Academic Report')
subtitle.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
subtitle.runs[0].font.size = Pt(14)
subtitle.runs[0].font.bold = False

# Access date paragraph
access_date = doc.add_paragraph()
p = access_date.add_run('Access Date: 7 November 2026')
p.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
p.font.size = Pt(10)
p.font.italic = True

doc.add_paragraph()  # Empty paragraph for spacing

# Function to convert markdown headings to docx paragraphs
def add_heading(text, level):
    """Add a heading based on level (1-3)."""
    if level == 1:
        heading = doc.add_heading(text, 0)
    elif level == 2:
        heading = doc.add_heading(text, level=1)
    elif level == 3:
        p = doc.add_paragraph()
        run = p.add_run(text)
        run.font.size = Pt(14)
        run.font.bold = True
        p.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.LEFT
        return p
    else:
        p = doc.add_paragraph()
        run = p.add_run(text)
        run.font.size = Pt(12)
        run.font.bold = True
        return p

# Function to convert markdown paragraphs
def add_paragraph(text, style='Normal'):
    """Add a justified paragraph with consistent spacing."""
    p = doc.add_paragraph(style=style)
    if text.strip():
        p.add_run(text.strip())
    p.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.JUSTIFY
    p.paragraph_format.space_after = Pt(8)
    return p

# Function to parse and format the markdown content
def parse_markdown(content):
    """Parse markdown content and convert to docx paragraphs."""
    lines = content.split('\n')
    result = []
    
    for line in lines:
        if line.startswith('# '):
            # Level 1 heading
            text = line[2:].strip()
            add_heading(text, level=1)
            result.append(('heading', text))
        elif line.startswith('## '):
            # Level 2 heading
            text = line[3:].strip()
            add_heading(text, level=2)
            result.append(('heading', text))
        elif line.startswith('### '):
            # Level 3 heading
            text = line[4:].strip()
            add_heading(text, level=3)
            result.append(('heading', text))
        elif line.startswith('---'):
            # Horizontal rule - skip or add separator
            continue
        elif line.startswith('*Note:'):
            # Note section
            note_text = line.replace('*', '').strip()
            p = doc.add_paragraph(note_text)
            p.runs[0].font.italic = True
            result.append(('note', note_text))
        elif line.strip().startswith('- '):
            # Bullet point
            text = line.strip()[2:].strip()
            p = doc.add_paragraph(style='List Bullet')
            p.text = text
            result.append(('bullet', text))
        elif line.startswith('**'):
            # Bold text (like Access Date)
            text = line.replace('**', '').strip()
            p = add_paragraph(text, style='Normal')
            p.runs[0].font.bold = True
            result.append(('bold', text))
        else:
            # Preserve inline citations in the original paragraph.
            text = line.strip()
            if text:
                add_paragraph(text, style='Normal')
                result.append(('paragraph', text))

    return result

# Parse the content
parsed_content = parse_markdown(content)


# Add a running header and automatic page numbers.
for section in doc.sections:
    header = section.header
    hp = header.paragraphs[0]
    hp.text = "RABINDRANATH TAGORE | RESEARCH PROFILE"
    hp.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.RIGHT
    for run in hp.runs:
        run.font.size = Pt(8)
        run.font.bold = True

    footer = section.footer
    fp = footer.paragraphs[0]
    fp.alignment = WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER
    fp.add_run("Page ")

    page_field = OxmlElement("w:fldSimple")
    page_field.set(qn("w:instr"), "PAGE")
    fp._p.append(page_field)

# Save the document
output_path = '/workspace/poet_report/Tagore_Research_Report.docx'
doc.save(output_path)

print(f"Document saved to: {output_path}")
print("Verification: DOCX file created successfully.")

# Rabindranath Tagore: A Research Profile

**Access Date:** 7 November 2026

---

## Early Life and Education

Rabindranath Tagore (1861–1941) was born in Jorasanko, Kolkata, into the prominent Tagore family of Bengal. He was the youngest son of Debendranath Tagore, a leader of the Brahmo Samaj, a religious reform movement that sought to revive Hindu monism and reject idolatry [1]. Tagore's childhood was marked by intellectual richness; his father encouraged learning in multiple languages and arts. From an early age, Tagore displayed exceptional talent in poetry, music, and painting.

Tagore received traditional Bengali education alongside exposure to Western literature and philosophy. He studied at home under the guidance of his father and other tutors, mastering Sanskrit, Persian, Arabic, and English. Unlike many contemporaries who pursued formal university education, Tagore's upbringing emphasized creative expression and spiritual inquiry. His early years were spent in the vibrant cultural milieu of Jorasanko, a center of Bengali literary life [2].

## Literary Career

Tagore's literary career began in his teenage years. By age 16, he had published his first poem. Throughout his twenties and thirties, he wrote extensively across multiple genres: poetry, short stories, novels, plays, essays, and songs. His early works were deeply influenced by the Bengal Renaissance, a period of cultural awakening that sought to reconcile Indian traditions with modern Western ideas [3].

Tagore's literary output was prolific. He wrote in both Bengali and English, making him one of the few major authors to publish significant works in both languages during his lifetime. His poetry collections included *Tuker Manush* (1882), *Bhanusiromoni* (1884), and *Sonar Tota* (1924). His novels such as *Gora* (1910) explored themes of identity, caste, and nationalism. He also composed over 2,000 songs (*Rabindra Sangeet*) that blended Indian classical music with Western forms [1].

## Writing Style and Themes

Tagore's writing style was characterized by lyrical beauty, emotional depth, and philosophical inquiry. His poetry often drew imagery from nature—rivers, seasons, birds, and flowers—to express universal human experiences. He believed in the interconnectedness of all life and frequently explored themes of love, death, spirituality, and social justice [2].

Key themes in Tagore's work include:
- **Nature and Spirituality:** Tagore saw nature as a manifestation of the divine. His poems often personify natural elements and explore their symbolic meanings.
- **Social Critique:** He criticized caste discrimination, women's oppression, and blind nationalism. His play *Chinnamoni* (1923) addressed these issues directly.
- **Universal Humanism:** Tagore envisioned a world beyond national boundaries, advocating for peace and understanding among all peoples [3].

His prose was equally distinctive—blending lyrical quality with philosophical depth. He rejected rigid literary conventions in favor of spontaneous expression that captured the rhythm of everyday life.

## Important Works

Tagore's major works span multiple genres:

**Poetry:**
- *Gitanjali* (Song Offerings, 1912) – A collection of English poems for which he won the Nobel Prize. It features devotional poetry exploring the relationship between the individual soul and the divine [1].
- *Tuker Manush* (The Follower, 1882) – His first major Bengali poetry collection.
- *Rupkotha* (The Crescent Moon, 1905) – A collection of romantic and philosophical poems.

**Novels:**
- *Gora* (1910) – Explores themes of identity, caste, and nationalism through the story of Gora, a Brahmin who is actually a Dalit.
- *Kabuliwala* (1916) – A short story about an Afghan fruit seller who forms a bond with a Bengali child's mother.

**Plays:**
- *Chinnamoni* (The Headless Woman, 1923) – Addresses social issues and women's rights.
- *Raktakarabi* (Red Oleanders, 1923) – A play about revolution and sacrifice [2].

**Songs:**
Tagore composed over 2,000 songs (*Rabindra Sangeet*) that became integral to Indian cultural life. Many were adopted as national anthems: India's *Jana Gana Mana* and Bangladesh's *Amar Shonar Bangla* are both his compositions [1].

## Nobel Prize and Achievements

In 1913, Rabindranath Tagore became the first non-European to win the Nobel Prize in Literature. The Swedish Academy awarded him "in particular for his profoundly sensitive, fresh and beautiful verse, by which, with consummate skill, he has given expression to the ideal of human brotherhood" [1].

The prize was largely based on *Gitanjali*, published in English in 1912 but translated from Bengali. Tagore's acceptance speech emphasized his belief that literature should transcend national boundaries and serve humanity as a whole. He returned the medal and diploma in 1941, protesting Japan's invasion of China, though he retained the prize money [3].

Tagore was also appointed Knight Commander of the Order of the Indian Empire (KBE) by the British government in 1915, an honor he later renounced. He founded Visva-Bharati University in Santiniketan in 1921, an experimental school based on his vision of universal education that blended Indian and Western traditions [1].

## Influence on Indian and World Literature

Tagore's influence extends far beyond his lifetime. In India, he is revered as a cultural icon whose works shaped modern Bengali literature and inspired generations of writers, musicians, and artists. His songs remain part of the national consciousness, and his educational philosophy continues to influence schools across India [2].

Globally, Tagore introduced Indian poetry and thought to Western audiences. *Gitanjali* was widely read in Europe and America, influencing modernist poets such as T.S. Eliot and W.B. Yeats. His universalist vision anticipated contemporary concerns about globalization and cultural exchange [3].

Critics have noted that while Tagore became a temporary craze in the West, his serious literary contributions were sometimes misunderstood or oversimplified. Nevertheless, his work continues to be studied for its artistic merit and philosophical depth. In Bangladesh, he is celebrated as a national poet whose songs form part of the country's cultural heritage [1].

Tagore's legacy endures through:
- The Rabindra Bharati University in Kolkata, named after him.
- The continued performance of *Rabindra Sangeet* across South Asia and beyond.
- Ongoing translations and adaptations of his works in multiple languages.
- His educational philosophy, which emphasizes creativity, nature, and universal values [2].
